We then train four restricted visual prompts using supervised learning followed by conditional direct preference optimisation, while keeping the VLM backbone frozen. The resulting models achieve balanced accuracies of 70.6% for ICU admission and 67.5% for deterioration and increase the matched-versus-mismatched performance gap to approximately 16.5 and 5.5 percentage points, respectively. Overall, our study identifies ECG Mirage in multimodal clinical prediction and introduces visual prompt tuning as an efficient mitigation strategy.

</details>


### [179] [LLM-Generated Feature Pools for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21801)
*Youssef Attia El Hili,Malik Tiomoko,Corinne Ancourt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单统计流程用于单变量时间序列异常检测，并通过多模态大模型生成候选统计特征池，最终在TSB-AD-U上达到0.588 VUS-PR，追平榜单最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在严格选择协议下，简单统计流程在单变量时间序列异常检测上能达到何种水平，并识别性能瓶颈究竟来自特征搜索还是候选特征池本身。

Method: 在滑动窗口上提取一小组统计量，用转导式鲁棒（MAD）模型对每个窗口打分，并在留出的调参集上按域选择特征子集；进一步通过提示多模态大模型并给出该域的上下文示例窗口，为每个域生成候选统计特征池。

Result: 在TSB-AD-U上达到0.529每序列VUS-PR，高于公开榜单最佳神经（0.45）与统计（0.44）方法，距最强预训练基础模型仅0.06。消融显示：选择策略仅带来0.031波动，聚合网格0.096，而更换候选池达0.226。生成池与手工池在匹配选择下相当，二者并集在全部十二组生成器-种子对上均优于生成池，将流程提升至0.588，追平榜单最佳。

Conclusion: 候选特征池决定性能上限，池内搜索只是二阶因素；用多模态大模型按域生成候选池可有效替代手工设计，且与手工池互补，组合后达到榜单最佳水平。

Abstract: We study how far a simple statistical pipeline can go on univariate time series anomaly detection under a strict selection protocol. The method extracts a small pool of statistics over sliding windows, scores each window with a transductive robust (MAD) model, and selects a feature subset per domain on a held-out tuning split. On TSB-AD-U it reaches $0.529$ per-series VUS-PR, above the best neural ($0.45$) and statistical ($0.44$) entries on the public leaderboard and within $0.06$ of the strongest pretrained foundation model, several of which use more supervision than ours. Ablations locate the cause: across three selection strategies and a hindsight oracle the score moves by $0.031$, and across the aggregation grid by $0.096$, while changing the candidate pool moves it by $0.226$. The candidate pool sets the ceiling; the search over it is second-order. We therefore generate a pool per domain by prompting a multimodal LLM with in-context example windows from that domain. The generated pools match the hand-crafted one under matched selection, and the two cover different domains: selecting over their union improves on the generated pool in all twelve generator-seed pairs and lifts the pipeline to $0.588$, matching the performance of the best entry on the leaderboard.

</details>


### [180] [MIST: Multimodal Survival Prediction with Genomic-Guided Histology Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21811)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Richard R. Chen,Jana Lipkova,Benedikt Wiestler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIST，用基因组特征作为查询token引导组织学上下文注意力，实现多模态生存预测，在四个外部队列上提升C-index。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生存模型可融合全切片图像与基因组信息的互补预后价值，但面临外部队列分布漂移和计算复杂度高的融合难题，现有方法仅在最终阶段拼接独立编码的各模态，信息融合不充分。

Method: MIST将基因组特征表示为token，让其查询由基础模型衍生的紧凑组织学上下文token，再进行生存预测；训练结合离散时间生存预测、基因组特征掩码、WSI dropout以及配对的WSI-基因组对比对齐。

Result: 在结肠、肾、肺和胶质母细胞瘤四个外部队列评估中，MIST在主要对比上相对标准融合基线提升了外部C-index。

Conclusion: 基因组引导的组织学注意力是一种紧凑且有效的多模态肿瘤结局预测策略，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal survival models can combine complementary prognostic information from whole-slide images and genomic profiles, but effective fusion remains challenging amid external cohort shift and computational complexity. To address these challenges, we propose MIST, multimodal survival prediction with genomic-guided histology attention. MIST represents genomic features as tokens and allows them to query compact foundation-model-derived histology context tokens before survival prediction. This design enriches molecular information with histology context rather than merging separately encoded modalities only at the final stage. Training combines discrete-time survival prediction with genomic feature masking, WSI dropout, and paired WSI-genomics contrastive alignment. Across four external evaluations in colon, renal, lung, and glioblastoma cohorts, MIST improves external C-index over standard fusion baselines in the primary comparisons. These results support genomic-guided histology attention as a compact and effective strategy for multimodal oncology outcome prediction. Our code is available at https://github.com/samiyavuuz/MIST .

</details>


### [181] [EnterpriseVal: Quantifying the Efficacy, Reliability and Value of Generative AI in the Enterprise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21841)
*Abbas Raza Ali,Muhammad Ajmal Siddiqui,Moona Zahid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EnterpriseVal，一个面向企业级用例的生成式 AI 评测与决策系统，旨在弥合"模型能力"与"业务可上线性"之间的测量鸿沟，并在全球性银行的三个工作流中开展试点验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型已能产出专家认可的专业交付物，但多数企业 GenAI 项目难见可衡量的业务效果，大量智能体项目预期被取消。作者认为这本质上是测量问题：公开基准回答"模型能做什么"，而部署决策需要回答"该工作流在本企业数据与控制条件下是否合适、可靠、安全且值得规模化"。因此需要一套用例级、冻结配置下、可直接支撑上线决策的评测体系。

Method: 提出 EnterpriseVal，包含五个组成部分：(i) 用例与冻结的社会—技术配置形式化规范，涵盖模型、提示、检索、工具、护栏与人工监督，并以自主性等级与后果层级共同确定评测强度；(ii) 覆盖保真度、效用、效率、可靠性、保障与监督的指标目录；(iii) 结合盲评专家判断与经校准的 LLM-as-judge 打分，并通过预测驱动推断进行统计放大的评分协议；(iv) 以可执行算法表述的两级阈值门，将带置信边界的指标向量映射为 REJECT/CONDITIONAL/SCALE 决策；(v) 将审核者捕获率作为可测参数的价值—风险模型。

Result: 在全球性银行的三个工作流试点中：信贷备忘录撰写任务上，最优模型的人工评分引用精确率达 88%、幻觉率 1.6%，分别优于 70% 与 5% 的门槛；在流程改造任务中，分析师精修工作量由估计的 27.4 小时/份降至 2.9 小时/份。论文明确区分了已确立结果、试点证据、所提系统与开放假设，并给出了完整验证所需的实验设计。

Conclusion: 企业 GenAI 的落地困境在很大程度上是评测与决策测量问题，而非单纯的模型能力问题。EnterpriseVal 通过用例级规范、多维指标、可扩展评分协议与阈值门决策算法，将评测直接对接部署决策，初步试点显示了可行性与潜在价值，但其完整有效性仍有待所提出的实验加以验证。

Abstract: Frontier language models now produce professional deliverables that expert graders judge to match human work on a substantial share of economically valuable tasks, yet most enterprise GenAI initiatives fail to show a measurable business effect and a large fraction of agentic projects are expected to be cancelled. We argue that this is substantially a measurement problem: public benchmarks answer "what can the model do?", whereas a deployment decision requires "is this workflow fit, reliable, safe and worth scaling - here, on our data, under our controls?". We present EnterpriseVal, a use-case-level evaluation system that closes this gap. It comprises (i) a formal specification of the use case and of the frozen socio-technical configuration under test, model, prompts, retrieval, tools, guardrails and human oversight, with an autonomy level and consequence tier that jointly set the required evaluation intensity; (ii) a metric catalogue spanning fidelity, utility, efficiency, reliability, assurance and oversight; (iii) a grading protocol that scales blinded expert judgement with calibrated LLM-as-judge scoring through prediction-powered inference; (iv) a two-tier threshold gate, stated as an executable algorithm, that maps metric vectors with confidence bounds to REJECT/CONDITIONAL/SCALE decisions; and (v) a value-and-risk model in which the reviewer catch rate is a measured parameter. We report a pilot across three workflows in a global bank. In credit-memo drafting, human-graded citation precision reached 88% and hallucination rate 1.6% for the best model against gates of 70% and 5%; in procedure transformation, analyst refinement effort fell from an estimated 27.4 to 2.9 hours per document. We separate established results, documented pilot evidence, the proposed system and open hypotheses, and specify the experiments required for full validation

</details>


### [182] [AutoRecLab: Describe the Experiment, Get the Code!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21863)
*Moritz Baumgart,Philipp Meister,Justus Krell,Michael Schmidt,Bela Gipp,Joeran Beel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoRecLab：一个基于自然语言提示的自主推荐系统实验平台，自动完成从需求解析到完整实验实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统研究中，将实验设计转化为可执行代码仍依赖人工、易出错，缺乏自动化手段。

Method: 提出 AutoRecLab，基于 Python 的自主 RecSys 实验室；从自然语言研究想法中推导实验需求，构建并验证原型，再迭代扩展为完整实验；结合 RAG 文档检索、静态类型验证与执行引导的树搜索。

Result: 自主实现了一项显式到隐式反馈转换研究；在六种算法、三个数据集的基线对比中，9 次运行有 8 次成功，使用 GPT-5.4-mini 时平均每次成本约 1 美元。

Conclusion: AutoRecLab 展示了利用 LLM 代理自动化推荐系统实验流程的可行性与低成本潜力。

Abstract: Empirical evaluation is central to recommender-systems (RecSys) research, but turning experimental designs into executable code remains a manual and error-prone task. We present AutoRecLab, a Python-based autonomous RecSys lab that automates RecSys experiments from natural-language prompts. Given a research idea, AutoRecLab derives explicit experiment requirements, builds and validates a prototype, and iteratively expands it into the requested full experiment. The workflow combines retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for documentation lookup, static type verification, and execution-steered tree search. In our demonstration, AutoRecLab autonomously implements an explicit-to-implicit feedback conversion study. In a baseline comparison across six algorithms and three datasets, 8 of 9 runs succeed at an average cost of approx- imately $1 per run with GPT-5.4-mini.

</details>


### [183] [What Should We Ask Next? Retrieval-Aware Question Learning under Partial Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21924)
*Lyucheng Qian,John Yuehan Zhang,Pingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAVEL，一种检索感知的在线强化学习框架，用于交互式行人重识别中的提问策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式检索在证据不完整时是一个序列化信息获取问题：智能体需要判断哪个问题能为下一轮检索更新带来最有用的证据。现有系统通过模仿候选问答对的离线排序来训练这一决策，但问题价值实际由回答内容及其对检索的下游影响决定。作者指出候选区分度与主观感知有用性对该目标仅提供弱监督信号，因此需要一种基于真实检索反馈的训练方式。

Method: RAVEL从监督式问题生成初始化，直接观测当前Top-4候选，并利用完整“问题-回答-检索”闭环中的排序反馈，通过在线强化学习优化提问策略。

Result: 在Interactive-PEDES数据集上，RAVEL在五轮交互中取得逐步增强的检索性能。进一步分析显示，RAVEL将提问预算重新分配到局部化的开放式属性上，这类问题能提供更有用的检索证据，并在初始困难的查询上带来最大增益。

Conclusion: 以检索排序反馈作为奖励的在线强化学习，优于离线模仿式提问策略，为交互式检索提供了更有效的监督信号。

Abstract: Interactive retrieval under partial evidence is a sequential information-acquisition problem: an agent must decide which question will create the most useful evidence for the next retrieval update. Existing systems train this decision by imitating an offline ordering of candidate QA pairs, although question value is determined by the response it elicits and its downstream effect on retrieval. We establish that candidate discriminativeness and perceived usefulness provide weak supervision for this objective, then introduce RAVEL, a retrieval-aware online reinforcement learning framework for interactive person re-identification. RAVEL initializes from supervised question generation, observes the current Top-4 candidates directly, and optimizes the question policy with rank feedback from the full question-answer-retrieval loop. Experiments on Interactive-PEDES show that RAVEL delivers progressively stronger retrieval performance across five interaction rounds. Further analysis shows that RAVEL reallocates the questioning budget toward localized open-ended attributes, which provide more useful retrieval evidence and yield the largest gains on initially difficult queries.

</details>


### [184] [AutoViewMem: Self-Configuring Orthogonal Views for Conversational Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21940)
*Zijie Cao,Xijun Qu,Zhicheng Gu,Xiaoshu Chen,Duanyang Yuan,Yanning Hou,Sihang Zhou,Jianxing Gong,Jian Huang,Yang Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoViewMem 通过"先表示后检索"的思路，把长期对话记忆在写入阶段组织成自配置、低重叠的语义视图，从而提升长程问答与个性化表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体的长期记忆系统多采用固定粒度或静态模式，难以处理偏好、事件、约束、时间更新等异质信息混合在同一表示中的情况，导致语义干扰，使 top-K 检索对噪声敏感、相关证据排名靠后。

Method: 提出 AutoViewMem 数据驱动框架：从交互轨迹中发现候选语义视图，选取紧凑且互补的视图集合，并用其指导写入阶段的结构化抽取，生成有来源依据的记忆；将语义解耦从检索时前移到写入时，使标准 top-K 相似度检索即可获得聚焦证据，无需显式路由或迭代检索；并施加离线整合以提升记忆的紧凑性与一致性。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 与 PersonaMem 基准上，使用 Qwen3-8B 与 Qwen3-14B 两种骨干模型，AutoViewMem 在长程问答与个性化任务上优于强记忆基线，同时保持简单的推理流程。

Conclusion: 以表示为先、写入时解耦长期记忆，是一种在不增加推理复杂度的前提下提升 LLM 智能体长期记忆效果的有效方案。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for large language model (LLM) agents to maintain consistency and personalization over extended interactions. Existing memory systems typically rely on fixed granularities or static schemas, but these designs struggle when heterogeneous information, such as preferences, events, constraints, and temporal updates, is embedded in a single mixed representation. The resulting semantic interference makes top-K retrieval sensitive to noise and often leaves relevant evidence poorly ranked. We present AutoViewMem, a data-driven framework that organizes long-term conversational memory into self-configuring, low-overlap semantic views before indexing. AutoViewMem discovers candidate views from interaction traces, selects a compact complementary view set, and uses these views to guide write-time structured extraction of provenance-grounded memories. This representation-first design moves semantic disentanglement from retrieval time to write time, allowing standard top-K similarity search to retrieve focused evidence without explicit routing or iterative retrieval. We further apply offline consolidation to improve memory compactness and consistency. Experiments on the LoCoMo and PersonaMem benchmarks, under both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B backbones, show that AutoViewMem improves long-horizon question answering and personalization over strong memory baselines while preserving a simple inference pipeline.

</details>


### [185] [Learning Cardiac Features: ECG Biometrics Across Time and~Exercise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21962)
*Luca Thiebaud,Paul Chauchat,Mustapha Ouladsine,Stéphane Delliaux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了在运动应激与跨会话变异条件下心电生物特征识别的鲁棒性，提出基于孪生ResNet与多导联晚期融合的方法，在压力测试与公开基准上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心电信号蕴含个体特异模式，可用于身份识别，并有助于保护敏感心脏数据及作为自监督学习的预训练任务。但现有研究多局限于单会话、静息状态，缺乏对时间与生理变化的鲁棒性验证。

Method: 采用孪生ResNet结合多导联晚期融合策略，在取自心肺运动试验的大规模心电数据集上训练，并使用兼顾运动与时间的评估协议，同时在公开基准（CYBHi）上进行评测。

Result: 在会话内静息到峰值运动条件下达到1.7%的等错误率（EER），在CYBHi数据集上取得3.9%的当前最优结果。

Conclusion: 该首次综合评估表明，存在一种对生理与时间漂移具有鲁棒性的内在心脏特征信号，支持心电生物特征识别在真实场景下的可行性。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) carry subject-specific patterns enabling reliable individual discrimination, forming the basis of ECG biometrics. Beyond authentication, this paradigm holds significant potential to secure sensitive cardiac data and to serve as a pretext task in self-supervised learning. Yet, most studies remain confined to singlesession, resting data, leaving robustness to temporal and physiological variations largely untested. We address this gap by evaluating ECG biometrics under realistic conditions involving exercise-induced stress and cross-session variability. A Siamese ResNet with late multi-lead fusion strategy is trained on a large ECG dataset extracted from cardiopulmonary exercise tests and evaluated with a exercise-and time-aware protocol, as well as on public benchmarks. This first extensive assessment of ECG biometrics under combined physiological and temporal variability achieves an intra-session rest-to-peak EER of 1.7% and stateof-the-art 3.9% on the CYBHi dataset. Findings support the presence of an intrinsic cardiac signature resilient to physiological and temporal drift.

</details>


### [186] [A Lie Detector Test for Language Models: Reading Knowledge a Model Won't Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21996)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Probe of Internal Recognition (PIR)，通过读取模型内部状态识别其"认出的"正确答案，从而区分模型是"不愿答"还是"不能答"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能持有知识却不报告：可能在能力评估中"放水"（sandbagging），或给出与内部所知相反的答案。仅凭输出无法判断模型是隐藏答案还是根本不知道，这给能力评估、审计与遗忘验证带来困难。

Method: 借鉴取证学的隐藏信息测试（Concealed Information Test）：向嫌疑人呈现真实细节与干扰项，通过对其认出项的更强反应识别"有罪知识"。PIR将此机制迁移到模型内部：给出问题及其候选答案，从模型内部状态读出它认出哪个候选为正确。方法无需参考模型、无需标注真值语料（reference-free）。

Result: 在来自5个家族（Gemma、Qwen、Llama、Mistral、Phi）的8个模型上，PIR以0.70–0.87的平衡准确率恢复"被认出的答案"，显著高于未知项基线0.28–0.40与随机水平0.25。在提示欺骗、训练式放水、外部密码锁与电路破坏检查点等各种隐藏形式下仍可读，识别率0.85–0.93。模型隐藏已知答案时识别率保持高位；遗忘去除知识后，识别率降至模型从未知晓问题的水平。

Conclusion: PIR能够区分"不会回答"与"不能回答"的模型，支持放水审计与遗忘验证。该信号具有因果性，提供超越黑盒行为线索的信息，并可从选择题扩展到自由文本生成。

Abstract: Large language models can hold knowledge they do not report. A model may sandbag on a capability evaluation, or answer against what it internally knows, and its outputs alone cannot tell whether it is hiding an answer or simply does not have one. We borrow the Concealed Information Test, a forensic method that identifies guilty knowledge by presenting a suspect with the true detail among plausible decoys and measuring a stronger response to the item they recognize. Our method, Probe of Internal Recognition (PIR), does the same inside a model. It presents a question with its candidate answers and reads, from the model's internal states, which candidate the model recognizes as correct. PIR is reference-free, needing no honest reference model and no labeled truth corpus. Across eight models from five families (Gemma, Qwen, Llama, Mistral, and Phi), PIR recovers the recognized answer at 0.70 to 0.87 balanced accuracy, well above the 0.28 to 0.40 unknown-item baseline and the 0.25 chance rate. It stays readable across every form of concealment we test, from prompted deception and trained sandbagging to external password-locked and circuit-broken checkpoints, with recognition between 0.85 and 0.93. When the model hides a known answer, recognition stays high. When unlearning removes the knowledge, recognition drops to the level of a question the model never knew. PIR therefore separates a model that will not answer from one that cannot, which supports sandbagging audits and unlearning verification. The signal is causal, adds information beyond black-box behavioral cues, and extends from multiple-choice questions to free-form generation.

</details>


### [187] [CodeMidas: Scaling Agentic Coding RL Environments from Code Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22068)
*Bowen Ye,Lei Li,Shicheng Li,Zihao Yue,Linghao Zhang,Hanglong Lv,Yuanxin Liu,Wenhan Ma,Hao Tian,Rang Li,Jinhao Dong,Yikai Zhao,Xiangwei Deng,Hailin Zhang,Liang Zhao,Qi Liu,Lingpeng Kong,Tong Yang,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CodeMidas，一种仅以源代码为输入的智能体流水线，把开源代码库中已实现的功能转化为可执行的强化学习环境，构建了 5,545 个训练任务的数据集，训练后显著提升多种编程智能体基准表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练编程智能体需要大量且带可靠验证器的任务，而现有方法主要依赖 issue、commit 等开发过程产物来挖掘任务，可提取的任务范围受限，难以规模化构建 RL 环境。

Method: CodeMidas 在环境构建的每个阶段都分配智能体算力：智能体探索已实现功能以形成行为规约，基于原始代码的执行来构造测试，并通过执行检查和多次解答采样来验证与过滤候选任务；任务特定输入仅为源代码。

Result: 数据集包含来自 3,185 个开源代码库、覆盖 23 种编程语言与 15 个技术领域的 5,545 个训练任务。用 GRPO 训练 MiMo-V2.5 后，在五个基准上均有提升：问题修复 DeepSWE +11.7%、整程序构建 ProgramBench +17%、终端任务 Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%。消融显示高质量任务数量增加可提升性能；轨迹分析显示模型表现出更强的代码库探索和更多样的自验证行为。

Conclusion: 源代码可作为规模化构建 RL 环境的可靠基础，能够跨多种软件任务提升编程智能体能力。

Abstract: Training capable coding agents via reinforcement learning (RL) requires diverse tasks with reliable verifiers. Open-source codebases offer a rich source of such tasks, while existing methods typically rely on development artifacts such as issues and commits, limiting the range of tasks that can be extracted. To better scale RL environments, we present CodeMidas, an agentic pipeline that turns implemented functionality in existing codebases into executable RL environments using source code as its only task-specific input. CodeMidas allocates agentic compute to every stage of environment construction: agents explore implemented functionality to formulate behavioral specifications, construct tests grounded in execution of the original code, and validate and filter candidate tasks through execution checks and repeated solution rollouts. The resulting dataset has 5,545 training tasks from 3,185 open-source codebases spanning 23 programming languages and 15 technical domains. Training MiMo-V2.5 on these tasks with GRPO improves performance on all five diverse benchmarks, covering issue repair (DeepSWE + 11.7%), whole-program construction (ProgramBench +17%), and terminal work (Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%). Ablations show that increasing the number of high-quality training tasks improves performance. Trajectory analysis shows the RL-trained agent demonstrates better behaviors like increasing codebase exploration and more diverse self-verification. These results establish source code as a scalable foundation for constructing RL environments that improve coding agents across diverse software tasks.

</details>


### [188] [Designer-RSI: Evolving Procedural Memory from User Traffic for Agentic Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22086)
*Hongyang Du,Lan Yan,Christian Flores,Asim Kadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种持续适应框架：冻结前沿模型通过230+工具操作专业设计软件，外部程序性记忆积累并精炼自然语言设计技能，在无权重更新、无人工标注下将执行成功率从72.7%提升至99.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业平面设计是长时程智能体任务，结构化可编辑产物的生成依赖大量相互依赖的操作，且结果缺乏可靠的程序化评判标准（无可验证反馈），因此难以通过传统训练或人工标注方式持续提升智能体能力。

Method: 冻结的前沿模型通过230多个工具操控专业设计软件；构建外部自然语言技能程序性记忆。记忆通过两种机制演化：加宽（为反复出现但未被覆盖的子任务新增程序）与加深（依据自身成功/失败执行记录修订已有程序）；并设置匹配重放门控，只接受能修复失败且不损害已验证成功的改动。全过程无权重更新、无人工标签。

Result: 在1,406条真实用户brief与1,869条自动评分轨迹上进行五轮迭代，技能库从76条（源自文档）扩展至139条；Claude-Sonnet-4上GenEval2执行成功率由72.7%升至99.3%，生成质量+11.99分；在四个专业设计基准上对无技能智能体的胜率为61.8%（Sonnet-4）和67.6%（Opus-4.6）。在200条用户流量基准留出brief上，单独加宽/加深胜率为49.4%/48.6%，二者结合达58.5%（p=0.025），证明两机制互补有效。

Conclusion: 程序性记忆为智能体在噪声大、不可验证反馈条件下的持续适应提供了可行路径，无需权重更新或人工标注即可显著提升长时程专业任务的执行与生成质量。

Abstract: Professional graphic design is a long-horizon agentic task in which structured, editable artifacts emerge from many interdependent actions, yet outcomes admit no reliable programmatic oracle. We introduce a continual adaptation framework in which a frozen frontier model operates professional design software through more than 230 tools, while an external procedural memory of natural-language skills accumulates and refines reusable design procedures from experience. The memory widens by acquiring procedures for recurring uncovered subtasks and deepens by revising existing procedures against their own successful and failed executions, while a matched replay gate admits only changes that repair failures without regressing observed successes. Five rounds over 1,406 real user briefs and 1,869 automatically graded trajectories, with no weight updates and no human labels, grow the bank from 76 documentation-derived skills to 139 and raise GenEval2 execution success on Claude-Sonnet-4 from 72.7% to 99.3% (+11.99 points in generation quality), with 61.8% and 67.6% win rates against the no-skill agent across four specialized design benchmarks on Claude-Sonnet-4 and Claude-Opus-4.6. We further show the two mechanisms are effective in combination: on 200 held-out briefs from user-traffic benchmark, widening or deepening alone reaches a 49.4% / 48.6% win rate over the no-skill agent, while their combination reaches 58.5% (p = 0.025). Procedural memory offers a practical route to continual adaptation of agents under noisy, unverifiable feedback.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Sparse Priors for Efficient Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20883)
*Saumya Goyal,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"稀疏先验"类与"Sparse Dimension"概念，证明在k-稀疏先验下分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界为Ω(√(k/n))，并在TV距离下给出匹配上界，从而克服对样本量n的维数灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI的分布学习理论保证随维度d呈O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})退化，虽为极小极大最优但过于悲观；作者认为仅靠光滑性假设不足以刻画实际分布结构，需引入稀疏性来更准确地描述真实应用中的分布。

Method: 引入稀疏先验类，定义"稀疏维度"(Sparse Dimension)以度量先验在所有分布空间上的稀疏程度；在k-稀疏先验下推导贝叶斯风险下界，并在温和附加假设下给出TV距离的匹配上界；同时证明贝叶斯框架下分布学习与"学习采样"的统计等价性。

Result: 在常见距离度量下得到Ω(√(k/n))的贝叶斯风险下界；对TV距离，在温和假设下得到（渐近于n,k，忽略对数项）的匹配上界；该结果同样适用于学习采样任务。

Conclusion: 尽管k仍可能依赖维度d或内在维数，但结果表明在合适的先验下学习可克服关于样本量n的维数灾难，说明稀疏先验能比单纯光滑性假设更准确地刻画真实分布的可学习性。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use and success of generative AI techniques today, theoretical guarantees on learning a distribution supported in $d$ dimensions from $n$ samples degrade as $O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})$, though shown to be minimax optimal. We hypothesize that present bounds are too pessimistic because smoothness assumptions are not enough to capture the structure of distributions that often appear in real applications. Consequently, we introduce the class of sparse priors and define the "Sparse Dimension" as a measure of sparsity of a prior over the space of all distributions. We show that distribution learning under a $k$-sparse prior achieves a Bayesian risk lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{k/n})$ under common distance metrics, and show a matching (up to logarithmic terms asymptotically in $n,k$) upper bound for the TV distance under mild additional assumptions. We show the statistical equivalence of distribution learning and learning to sample in the Bayesian setting so that our results apply to learning to sample as well. While $k$ can still depend on the dimension $d$, or a notion of intrinsic dimension, our results show that learning under an appropriate prior overcomes the curse of dimensionality with respect to the dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [190] [BI-Agent and BI-Bench: Towards Automating End-to-End Business Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20886)
*Chuxuan Hu,Yeye He,Penny Zhou,Wee Hyong Tok,Daniel Kang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个端到端商业智能（BI）基准 BI-Bench，发现前沿LLM准确率不足50%，设计工具增强的 BI-Agent 并结合领域后训练，显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BI工作流要求用户手动完成识别相关表、数据转换、构建连接关系等繁琐的准备工作，之后才能回答业务问题。这些步骤复杂耗时，而LLM在数据处理上具备强大能力，因此作者希望研究LLM能否端到端地完成BI问答，免除用户的手工准备。

Method: 作者从公开来源收集真实BI项目，并从真实用户仪表盘中人工抽取（问题，标准答案）对，构建首个系统研究端到端BI能力的基准 BI-Bench。针对LLM表现不足的问题，设计了工具增强的 BI-Agent，将BI工作流分解为结构化数据上的子任务（如搜索、连接、转换），并编排各BI阶段的专用数据管理方法；同时提出后训练框架，从真实BI项目合成训练轨迹，对 BI-Agent 进行监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL）训练。

Result: 即使是前沿LLM在 BI-Bench 上表现也很差，准确率低于50%。BI-Agent 配合普通LLM可将准确率提升最多40个百分点，经过后训练的 BI-Agent 进一步提升最多30个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在复杂BI工作流中结合工具增强推理与领域特定后训练十分关键，并为未来研究指明了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) is a cornerstone of enterprise decision-making and is widely used by enterprise users in software such as Power BI and Tableau. In traditional BI workflows, users need to prepare data by (1) identifying relevant tables, (2) performing data transformations, and (3) building join relationships, before they can (4) answer their business questions. These steps can be complex and time-consuming, making BI challenging.
  Given the strong capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in working with data, we study their ability to answer BI questions end-to-end, without requiring users to manually perform the tedious preparation steps. To do this, we harvest a large collection of real-world BI projects from public sources, and manually extract pairs of (questions, ground-truth answers) from real user dashboards. The resulting benchmark, BI-Bench, is the first benchmark to systematically study LLMs' ability on end-to-end BI.
  We find that even frontier LLMs perform poorly on BI-Bench, with less than 50% accuracy. To address their limitations, we design a tool-augmented BI-Agent that decomposes BI workflows into subtasks on structured data, such as search, join, and transform, and orchestrates specialized data management methods across BI stages. Furthermore, we develop a post-training framework that synthesizes training trajectories from real BI projects, enabling BI-Agent to be further post-trained using both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). BI-Agent achieves substantial accuracy gains of up to 40 percentage points with vanilla LLMs, and post-trained BI-Agent yields gains of up to 30 points. Our results highlight the importance of combining tool-augmented reasoning with domain-specific post-training in complex BI workflows, and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [191] [Elastic Threshold Attention: Learned Contextual Sparsity for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20888)
*Themistoklis Haris,Henry Li,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ETA 是一种可端到端训练的稀疏注意力架构，通过从查询表示预测动态上下文阈值，在保持稠密模型质量的同时实现硬件加速解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中，庞大的 KV 缓存造成严重的内存带宽瓶颈；现有稀疏注意力方法依赖刚性启发式规则，会丢弃必要上下文，导致质量下降。

Method: 提出 Elastic Threshold Attention（ETA）：1）直接由查询表示预测动态上下文阈值；2）训练时对低于阈值的 logits 进行乘性抑制而非删除，形成平滑均匀注意力下限与分布式概率储备；3）推理时对无信息 KV 块进行硬剪枝，并吸收粗粒度 GPU 块选择带入的附带 token；4）在 Triton 中实现自定义解码内核，利用缓存的几何-概率边界以 O(1) 时间筛选 KV 块；5）提出离线校准算法，冻结每头常量阈值以消除预测器开销。

Result: 1.45B 预训练 ETA 模型在语言建模、常识推理和长上下文检索上媲美稠密注意力（约 85% 训练稀疏度、约 38% 活跃解码密度）；解码速度较 FlashAttention-2 提升最高 2.5 倍（序列长至 512K）；离线校准可再减少 27% 注意力计算量。

Conclusion: ETA 证明了可学习的动态阈值稀疏注意力能在不牺牲质量的前提下显著加速长上下文解码，并通过训练策略消除了初始 token 上的注意力汇聚现象。

Abstract: Massive KV caches can cause severe memory-bandwidth bottlenecks during long-context decoding. Sparse attention methods mitigate this via selective loading, but that comes at a cost: rigid heuristics drop necessary context, leading to quality degradation. We introduce \textbf{Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)}, an end-to-end trainable architecture that achieves hardware-accelerated decoding speed without sacrificing dense model quality. ETA predicts dynamic, contextual thresholds directly from query representations, allowing the model to allocate dense-like context to difficult retrieval or reasoning steps while pruning routine tokens. To learn this policy from scratch without representation collapse, ETA \emph{multiplicatively suppresses} sub-threshold logits toward zero during training rather than deleting them. Training against this smooth uniform attention floor provides a distributed probability reservoir that \textbf{causes localized attention sinks on initial tokens to disappear}. It also enables the model to hard-prune uninformative KV blocks at inference time and absorb incidental tokens co-admitted by coarse GPU block selection. As a result, a 1.45B pretrained ETA model rivals dense attention across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and long-context needle retrieval at $\approx 85\%$ training sparsity and $\approx 38\%$ active decode density. At inference time, we implement a custom decode kernel in Triton that screens KV blocks in $O(1)$ time using cached geometric-probabilistic bounds, delivering up to $2.5\times$ wall-clock decode speedups over FlashAttention-2 on sequences up to 512K tokens. Finally, we introduce an offline calibration algorithm for domain-specific deployments that freezes per-head constant thresholds to eliminate predictor overhead, cutting attention compute by an additional $27\%$.

</details>


### [192] [Bio-MF: Low-Latency and High-Fidelity EEG-to-fNIRS Cross-Modal Generation for Hybrid Motor-Imagery Brain--Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20904)
*Boyuan Zhao,Sifan Zhang,Luping Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Bio-MF：一种无潜变量、单步 MeanFlow 框架，用于 EEG 条件生成 fNIRS 信号，实现快速且保真的跨模态合成以支持混合 MI-BCI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG-fNIRS 混合脑机接口性能优于纯 EEG 系统，但当配对采集不可得或不方便时，需要 EEG 到 fNIRS 的跨模态生成。现有方法生成速度慢且常需预训练，难以满足实时 MI-BCI 需求；而一步生成模型虽低延迟，却因去除迭代精修而降低保真度并引入非生理伪影。

Method: Bio-MF 采用无潜变量的单步 MeanFlow 框架：直接在信号空间做 x-prediction，并将该信号空间输出转换为 MeanFlow 速度监督，推理仅需一次网络评估。为在异构传感器布局下保留任务相关血流动力学结构，集成时空交互 4D 编码、跨模态无分类器引导以及噪声级门控 FFT 正则化。

Result: 数据集 1 上，EEG+合成 fNIRS 相对纯 EEG 的 ACC 分别提升 3.37（HbR）和 4.15（HbO）个百分点；数据集 2 在未见过的 64 通道 EEG 蒙太奇下提升 2.98 和 2.50 个百分点。在 RTX PRO 6000 GPU 上，生成一个 fNIRS trial 仅需 7.0 ms，相比 1000 步 SCDM 延迟提速 857 倍。

Conclusion: Bio-MF 实现了快速 EEG-to-fNIRS 合成，同时保持任务相关的生成质量，可用于下游混合 MI 解码。代码已开源。

Abstract: Hybrid motor-imagery brain-computer interfaces (MI-BCIs) combining EEG and fNIRS can outperform EEG-only systems by exploiting complementary electrophysiological and hemodynamic information. To obtain such hybrid information when paired EEG-fNIRS acquisition is unavailable or inconvenient, recent studies have focused on EEG-to-fNIRS cross-modal generation. However, existing methods still suffer from slow generation and often require pretraining, limiting their use in real-time MI-BCI scenarios. Although one-step generative models offer an attractive route to low-latency synthesis, removing the iterative refinement process can reduce generation fidelity and introduce non-physiological artifacts. To address these problems, this paper proposes Bio-MF, a latent-free one-step MeanFlow framework for EEG-conditioned fNIRS generation. Bio-MF performs direct signal-space x-prediction, converts this signal-space output into MeanFlow velocity supervision, and completes inference with one network evaluation. To preserve task-relevant hemodynamic structure under heterogeneous sensor layouts, Bio-MF integrates Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding, cross-modal classifier-free guidance, and noise-level-gated FFT regularization. On Dataset 1, EEG + synthetic fNIRS improves ACC over EEG-only by 3.37 and 4.15 percentage points for HbR and HbO, respectively. On Dataset 2, the corresponding gains remain 2.98 and 2.50 percentage points under the unseen 64-channel EEG montage. On an RTX PRO 6000 GPU, Bio-MF generates one fNIRS trial in 7.0 ms, corresponding to an 857x speedup over the 1000-step SCDM latency. These results show that Bio-MF enables fast EEG-to-fNIRS synthesis while preserving task-relevant generation quality for downstream hybrid MI decoding. Our code is available at https://github.com/psychosiwa/Bio-MF.

</details>


### [193] [Continuous Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent and Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning from History of Astrophysical Time Series Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20906)
*Debartha Paul,Juncheng Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文综述了用神经网络的随机微分方程（SDE）建模类星体光变曲线，并提出带延迟记忆的连续型随机梯度下降及连续时间策略梯度的强化学习结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类星体亮度随机变化蕴含超大质量黑洞信息，但从地面巡天光变曲线时间序列中建模这些变化是统计难题，需要借助SDE与神经网络参数化方法来解决。

Method: 回顾SDE结合神经网络参数化的发展历史；提出连续延迟记忆随机梯度下降（依赖离散迭代过程的过去状态）；在二维景观上进行仿真并调节超参数；提出无需求解HJB偏微分方程的连续时间策略梯度强化学习结构。

Result: 相比Vanilla SGD，所提方法在二维景观上表现出更广的探索范围和更精确的收敛行为；连续时间策略梯度的最优性条件可恢复先前工作中的Gibbs策略。

Conclusion: SDE与神经网络参数化的结合为类星体光变建模及连续时间优化与强化学习提供了有效框架，带延迟记忆的SGD与连续时间策略梯度均展现出理论优势。

Abstract: Quasars are luminous objects in the universe that exhibit stochastic brightness variations encoding information about the supermassive black holes powering them, and modeling these variations from ground-based survey data time series, known as light curves, is a statistical challenge. This paper reviews how stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have been adapted with neural network parameterizations to overcome this challenge in history. We create the Continuous-Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent which depend on the past state of the discrete iteration process. We performed the simulation on some 2-dimensional landscape and observed some wider-exploration and more precise convergent behavior compared to Vanilla SGD by adjusting hyperparameters. Besides, we proposed a reinforcement learning structure with continuous time policy gradients for exploratory policies without solving HJB PDE, and we show that its optimality conditions recover the Gibbs policy of previous works.

</details>


### [194] [Do Quantum Models Scale Like LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20912)
*David S. Berman,Ying-Jer Kao,Roger G. Melko,Alexander G. Stapleton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究RydbergGPT的神经缩放定律，发现临界点附近损失随数据量的幂律描述更好，且临界统计与自然语言最接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经缩放定律是否依赖于模型-数据对的统计特性，尤其是数据是否具有多尺度依赖性。作者利用Rydberg原子阵列的量子测量数据，检验临界点附近的多尺度统计是否能带来更稳定的神经缩放行为，并与自然语言语料对比。

Method: 训练自回归Transformer模型RydbergGPT，数据来自相互作用Rydberg原子阵列的量子比特投影测量。通过改变激光失谐参数使系统处于临界点附近或远离临界点，分析损失随训练数据规模的缩放关系，并采用熵归一化、有限样本校正的互信息"两点"函数比较Rydberg测量数据与自然语言语料的统计结构。

Result: 在临界点附近，损失随训练数据量的变化可由带损失下限修正的幂律很好地描述；远离临界点时幂律描述质量显著下降。临界点附近的两点函数统计与自然语言最接近，而远离临界点的量子比特构型两点函数衰减更快。

Conclusion: 多尺度依赖性有助于稳定的神经缩放，缩放行为应被视为模型-数据对的属性，而非仅由模型或数据单方面决定。

Abstract: In this work, we study the neural scaling laws of RydbergGPT, an autoregressive transformer model trained on qubit projective measurement data gathered from interacting Rydberg atom arrays. The quantum system is known to exhibit a finite-size remnant of a critical point as the laser detuning parameter is varied. We find that near the critical point the transformer loss as a function of training dataset size is well described by a power-law with a loss floor correction. However, away from criticality the quality of the power-law description is substantially reduced. We then compare the statistical structure of both Rydberg measurements and natural-language corpora using an entropy-normalised, finite sample corrected mutual information "two-point" function. We find that near-critical statistics of the two point functions are closest to those observed in natural-language, whilst other qubit configurations far from the critical point have two-point functions that decay more rapidly. This supports the hypothesis that multi-scale dependence contributes to stable neural scaling, and that scaling behaviour should be viewed as a property of the model-data pair.

</details>


### [195] [When AI Reviews Train AI Reviewers: Scientific-Judgment Collapse and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20942)
*Sy-Tuyen Ho,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM作为审稿人时"AI同行评审递归化"的风险，发现用模型生成评审参与训练会导致评分分布压缩与语义多样性下降，并称之为"科学判断崩塌"，同时提出TrustReviewer系统进行缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地参与科学评审（自动评审或辅助人类评审）。由于模型生成的评审会进入公开数据和未来训练语料，AI同行评审可能形成递归反馈循环——后一代审稿模型会学习前一代模型的判断，这一潜在风险缺乏受控环境下的实证研究。

Method: 以Llama 3.1 8B为基座，先在2018–2023年ICLR官方评审上微调一个审稿模型，再用ICLR 2024数据、以系统变化比例的官方评审与模型生成评审混合，训练四个后继模型。在此基础上提出TrustReviewer：训练时在精选语料上单阶段训练核心审稿模型以减少低质量与语义退化监督；测试时采用配对激活引导（activation steering），在无需额外训练或专家标注的情况下进一步缓解崩塌倾向。

Result: 引入合成评审会压缩评分分布，并降低同一论文内部及语料库层面的语义多样性，作者将这一现象命名为"科学判断崩塌"（scientific-judgment collapse）。TrustReviewer的两阶段干预在实践中被用于保持判断多样性并改善推荐对齐。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了递归审稿模型训练的具体风险，并提出训练时预防与测试时校正相结合的实用干预手段，为AI辅助科学评审中保持判断多样性与提升推荐对齐提供了可行方案。该工作属于LLM范畴。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly participate in scientific evaluation, both as automated reviewers and as assistants to human reviewers. As model-generated reviews enter public data and future training corpora, AI peer review can become recursive: later reviewers learn from judgments produced by earlier models. We study one step of this feedback loop in a controlled setting. Starting from Llama 3.1 8B, we first fine-tune a reviewer on official ICLR reviews from 2018--2023 and then train four successor models on ICLR 2024 data with systematically varied mixtures of official and model-generated reviews. Our study shows that introducing synthetic reviews compresses rating distributions and reduces both same-paper and corpus-level semantic diversity. We call this pattern $\textbf{scientific-judgment collapse}$.
  To mitigate this failure mode, we introduce $\textbf{TrustReviewer}$, an open-source LLM-based system for generating peer reviews of AI and machine learning papers. TrustReviewer intervenes at two complementary stages. For training-time prevention, we train the core reviewer in a single stage on a curated corpus designed to reduce low-quality and semantically degenerate supervision. For test-time correction, paired activation steering aims to further mitigate residual tendencies toward collapsed judgments without further training or additional expert annotation. Together, these results characterize a concrete risk of recursive reviewer training and provide practical interventions for preserving judgment diversity and improving recommendation alignment in AI-assisted scientific evaluation.

</details>


### [196] [Efficient Bayes-Adaptive Reinforcement Learning with Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20954)
*Jonathan Hau,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端基于模型的强化学习算法，通过将LTL任务的LDBA与环境BAMDP同步，并设计新型BAMCP算法，实现未知环境下高效且安全的策略合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中满足线性时序逻辑（如安全、可达性）规范进行策略合成，传统无模型方法探索-利用权衡差、样本效率低，且训练过程中易产生任务违规。

Method: 将LTL规范的极限确定性Büchi自动机（LDBA）与环境的贝叶斯自适应马尔可夫决策过程（BAMDP）同步；借助贝叶斯RL改进探索-利用权衡；提出新的贝叶斯自适应蒙特卡洛规划（BAMCP）算法，在同步BAMDP结构上近似求解贝叶斯最优策略。

Result: 在有限与无限时域任务实验中，相比传统无模型方法，性质满足率和样本效率均更优；消融实验表明新BAMCP优于经典BAMCP；在谨慎RL场景中能减少训练期间的任务违规次数。

Conclusion: 同步LDBA与BAMDP并结合新型BAMCP，可有效实现未知环境下LTL任务的贝叶斯最优策略合成，并支持谨慎RL应用。

Abstract: We present a novel end-to-end model-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithm for efficient policy synthesis under given Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) specifications (e.g., safety or reachability) in unknown environments. To do so, a Limit-Deterministic B{ü}chi Automaton (LDBA) representation of the LTL task is synchronised with a Bayes-Adaptive Markov Decision Process (BAMDP) representation of the environment, which allows us to leverage an enhanced exploration-exploitation trade-off that is achieved via Bayesian RL, as opposed to traditional non-Bayesian approaches. We further propose a novel Bayes-Adaptive Monte-Carlo Planning (BAMCP) algorithm to allow for approximate Bayes-optimal strategy synthesis in the synchronised BAMDP construct. A range of finite- and infinite-horizon task experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in terms of both property satisfaction and sample efficiency, when compared to traditional model-free approaches. Additional ablation studies also successfully highlight the value of the novel BAMCP algorithm in comparison to classical BAMCP for LTL task satisfaction. Finally, we also showcase a successful application of our approach for \textit{cautious} RL, namely to reduce the number of task violations incurred during policy training.

</details>


### [197] [From Switching to Dynamic Regret: A Simple Reduction via Unbiased Random Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20968)
*Yibo Wang,Wenhao Yang,Sifan Yang,Yuanyu Wan,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将动态遗憾最小化归约为切换遗憾最小化的简单框架，用现成算法得到强凸、指数凹及一般凸损失下最优动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳在线学习中，动态遗憾的最优界（尤其强凸和指数凹损失）通常依赖复杂分析，缺乏统一且简洁的推导方法。

Method: 对任意比较器序列构造逐轮无偏、方差可控且切换次数可控的辅助随机序列，结合代理损失，将动态遗憾分解为对随机序列的期望切换遗憾与其受控方差。

Result: 强凸和指数凹损失达到 Õ(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3}) 动态遗憾界；一般凸损失恢复 O(√(T(1+P_T))) 界，三者均匹配 minimax 最优结果。

Conclusion: 该归约框架简洁通用，借助切换遗憾算法即可统一推导多种损失类型的最优动态遗憾界。

Abstract: In non-stationary online learning, dynamic regret has attracted increasing attention as a measure of how well an online learner performs against a time-varying comparator sequence. Despite considerable advances, attaining optimal bounds for strongly convex and exp-concave losses often involves intricate analysis. In this paper, we present a \textit{simple} framework that reduces dynamic regret minimization to switching regret minimization. As a result, we can derive dynamic regret bounds by using off-the-shelf algorithms with switching regret guarantees. The key idea of our reduction is to construct, for \textit{any} comparator sequence, an auxiliary random sequence that is unbiased at each round, with the controlled variance and a manageable number of switches. Combining this construction with suitable surrogate losses, we can decompose dynamic regret into the expected switching regret against the random sequence and its controlled variance. Theoretically, for strongly convex and exp-concave losses, we establish the $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})$ dynamic regret bounds, where $T$ denotes the time horizon and $P_T$ denotes the path-length of the comparator sequence. Moreover, for general convex losses, the same reduction also recovers the $O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})$ dynamic regret bound. Notably, all our findings match the minimax optimal results for these three types of losses, highlighting the versatility of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [198] [Generative inversion for early ranking of competing geologic interpretations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20978)
*Harun Ur Rashid,Daniel O'Malley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将竞争性地质解释转化为空间先验，并通过与水力水头观测的一致性进行排序的工作流，用于数据稀缺下的地下决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险地下决策常在数据严重稀缺条件下进行，专家对同一地下系统可能给出相互竞争的解释，早期缺乏客观判定哪种更合理的方法，这种不确定性可能持续到钻多口井之后，代价高达数百万美元。现有评估方法依赖主观判断或早期难以获得的密集数据。

Method: 将不同地质解释转化为替代性空间先验并按其与水头观测的一致性排序。对每个解释，用文本到图像基础模型生成1600张地质图像集合，用单独训练的变分自编码器提供解释专属潜表示；监督式逆网络将水头观测映射到该潜空间，冻结解码器生成图像并映射为对数导水率场；稳态流动模拟给出预测水头，将失配转换为高斯形式兼容性得分。

Result: 在基于Johansen组的合成基准上，对三种与参考表示一致性递减的解释进行评测。925个测试案例中，平均水头RMSE由Precise & Accurate解释的0.197升至Accurate的0.227、Mismatched的0.280。应用于Waste Isolation Pilot Plant的Culebra白云岩段两个已发表概念模型，修订模型兼容性权重0.991，原模型0.009，与独立证据一致。

Conclusion: 该工作流能在早期数据稀缺阶段客观区分并排序竞争性地质解释，其兼容性评分与独立证据相符，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: High-consequence subsurface decisions are often made under severe data scarcity. Experts may arrive at competing interpretations of the same subsurface system, yet early in a project there is rarely a practical way to determine which one is most realistic. This uncertainty can persist until several wells are drilled, often costing millions of dollars. Existing approaches for evaluating geologic interpretations rely either on subjective judgment or on dense data that are rarely available in early-stage investigations. We present a workflow that addresses this challenge by translating competing geologic interpretations into alternative spatial priors and ranking them according to their consistency with hydraulic-head observations. For each interpretation, a text-to-image foundation model generates an ensemble of 1600 geologic images, and a separately trained variational autoencoder provides an interpretation-specific latent representation. A supervised inverse network maps the head observations into this latent space, and the frozen decoder produces an image that is mapped to a log-conductivity field. Steady-state flow simulation then provides predicted heads, and the resulting mismatch is converted into a Gaussian-form compatibility score. We evaluate the framework using a synthetic benchmark based on the Johansen Formation and three interpretations of decreasing consistency with the reference representation. Across 925 test cases, the mean head RMSE increases from 0.197 for the Precise \& Accurate interpretation to 0.227 for the Accurate interpretation and 0.280 for the Mismatched interpretation. We subsequently apply the workflow to two published conceptual models of the Culebra Dolomite Member at the Waste Isolation Pilot Plant. The revised model receives a compatibility weight of 0.991, compared with 0.009 for the original model, consistent with the independent evidence.

</details>


### [199] [ASGARD: Action-Space Guard for UAV Resilience via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20982)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ASGARD两阶段师生框架，使基于强化学习的无人机控制能抵御动作空间攻击，并可在机载运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习控制器易受动作空间攻击（策略生成动作后、执行器执行前被篡改），而现有防御多针对策略输入攻击，或需在训练时重训策略，无法应对运行时被污染的动作。

Method: ASGARD采用两阶段师生流水线。教师阶段：编码器将无人机物理状态与动作攻击相关的特权信息结合，生成动作攻击感知的隐变量，用于训练RL控制策略及一个向执行器输出修正动作的监视器。学生阶段：编码器与监视器通过监督学习从教师对应部分蒸馏，仅依赖无人机物理状态历史即可在机载运行。

Result: 在针对无人机不同动作命令的多种攻击场景下评估，ASGARD能在攻击下完成任务，并对未见过的攻击具有泛化性，对隐蔽攻击也保持韧性。

Conclusion: ASGARD是一种无需重训策略、可机载部署的运行时防御方案，有效提升RL无人机控制对动作空间攻击的韧性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) controllers have been recently adopted for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV) navigation and control. However, they are susceptible to action-space attacks that overwrite the action commands after the policy generates them and before the actuators execute them. While most existing defenses target attacks on the policy's inputs, those addressing action-space attacks retrain the policy at training time and are not resilient to corrupted actions at runtime. We propose ASGARD, a two-phase teacher-student pipeline for making RL-based UAV control resilient to action-space attacks. In the teacher phase, an encoder combines the UAV's physical state with action-attack-related privileged information to produce an action-attack-aware latent that trains the RL control policy and a monitor that outputs corrected action commands to the actuators. In the student phase, both the encoder and the monitor are trained via supervised learning from their teacher counterparts to run on-board using only the UAV's physical state history. We evaluate ASGARD across attack scenarios targeting different action commands on UAV. We find that ASGARD is resilient to action-space attacks and completes the missions despite the attack. We further find that ASGARD generalizes to unseen attacks and remains resilient against stealthy attacks.

</details>


### [200] [From Stress to Affect: Multimodal Deep Learning for Physiological Emotion Recognition Across Wearable Sensor Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20991)
*Desta Haileselassie Hagos,Saurav Keshari Aryal,Legand L. Burge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对生理情感识别中的时序深度学习架构（LSTM、TCN、Transformer）在WESAD和EmoWear两个多模态可穿戴数据集上进行了系统性比较，考察了传感配置、集成、采样频率等因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理情感识别研究通常只评估单一模型、传感配置或数据集，难以理解这些因素如何影响识别性能，缺乏系统性的比较与选型指导。

Method: 采用WESAD和EmoWear两个多模态可穿戴数据集，在腕部、胸部及多模态三种传感配置下，用被试独立的留一被试交叉验证（LOSO-CV）评估双向LSTM、TCN和Transformer，并研究软投票集成、传感器消融、采样频率和基于梯度的显著性。

Result: Transformer在WESAD多模态上准确率最高（99.02%±0.51%）；LSTM在EmoWear上多模态表现最佳，唤醒91.80%±1.06%，效价89.96%±0.36%。多模态传感在两个数据集上均优于仅腕部或仅胸部；4 Hz采样在性能接近更高频率的同时显著降低训练成本。

Conclusion: 架构的相对性能取决于数据集特性而非某一种架构普遍更优，多模态传感具有一致优势，4 Hz是实用的采样工作点，为可穿戴生理情感识别的架构、传感模态和采样频率选择提供了指导。

Abstract: Physiological emotion recognition using wearable sensors has important applications in mental health monitoring, affective computing, and human-computer interaction. However, existing studies typically evaluate a single model, sensing configuration, or dataset, limiting our understanding of how these factors influence recognition performance. We present a comparative study of temporal deep learning architectures for physiological emotion recognition using two multimodal wearable datasets: WESAD and EmoWear. Bidirectional long short-term memory (LSTM), temporal convolutional network (TCN), and Transformer models are evaluated under wrist-only, chest-only, and multimodal sensing configurations using participant-independent leave-one-subject-out cross-validation (LOSO-CV). We also investigate soft-voting ensembles, sensor ablation, sampling frequency, and gradient-based saliency. The Transformer achieved the highest multimodal accuracy on WESAD (99.02% +/- 0.51%), whereas the LSTM achieved the best multimodal accuracy on EmoWear for both arousal (91.80% +/- 1.06%) and valence (89.96% +/- 0.36%). These results show that relative architecture performance depends on dataset characteristics rather than one architecture being uniformly superior. Multimodal sensing consistently outperformed wrist-only and chest-only configurations across both datasets. Sampling-frequency analysis showed that 4 Hz provides a practical operating point, with performance comparable to higher frequencies at substantially lower training cost. These findings provide guidance for selecting architectures, sensing modalities, and sampling frequencies for wearable physiological emotion recognition.

</details>


### [201] [MOSAIC-SR: Transformer-Guided Symbolic Regression for Scientific Equation Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20997)
*Peiyi Zheng,Yanming Kang,Hans De Sterck,Giang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC-SR结合预训练Transformer生成的多个初始表达式草图与局部搜索，在符号回归任务上取得最优的符号解恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归旨在从观测数据中恢复闭式方程，为科学发现提供可解释模型。现有方法难以兼顾灵活的结构搜索与高效推理：基于搜索的方法结构可精调，但依赖随机初始化与高代价组合优化；预训练神经模型虽能瞬间生成公式，却常含符号性错误。

Method: 提出MOSAIC-SR：用预训练Transformer提出多个初始表达式草图，将其作为搜索起点，避免在庞大表达式空间中随机初始化；每个搜索过程通过尺度感知的常数优化与局部符号修复，联合恢复表达式结构与常数。

Result: 在SRSD-Feynman数据集（含与不含哑变量）及六个额外基准上评测，MOSAIC-SR在所有数据集上取得最高的符号解率，同时预测精度排名前二；在存在无关哑变量输入时优势依然保持。

Conclusion: 学习到的先验可将搜索聚焦于有潜力的方程结构，而数值优化与符号修复对表达式恢复至关重要。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to recover closed-form equations from observations, providing interpretable models for scientific discovery. Existing approaches struggle to combine flexible structural search with efficient inference. Search-based methods can refine expression structure but often rely on costly combinatorial optimization with random initialization. Pretrained neural models generate formulas almost instantly, but their predictions often contain symbolic errors. We introduce MOSAIC-SR, which uses a pretrained Transformer to propose multiple initial sketches. These sketches initialize searches in several promising regions, avoiding random starts in the vast expression space. Each search jointly recovers structure and constants through scale-aware constant optimization and local symbolic repair. We evaluate MOSAIC-SR on the SRSD-Feynman dataset with and without dummy variables and on six additional benchmarks. MOSAIC-SR obtains the highest symbolic solution rate on every dataset while ranking among the top two methods in predictive accuracy. This advantage persists in the presence of irrelevant dummy inputs. The results show that learned priors can focus search on promising equation structures, and that numerical optimization and symbolic repair are important for recovery.

</details>


### [202] [On the Limits of Maximal Coding Rate Reduction for Out-of-Distribution Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21001)
*Menghui Zhou,Gaoshan Bi,Vitaveska Lanfranchi,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究MCR²目标的分布外泛化局限，证明其编码最优不等于预测稳定，且引入不变性原理也无法消除失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCR²被视为可解释白盒架构的信息论框架，但作者观察到它在分布偏移下会完全失败，因此需探究其OOD泛化极限。

Method: 通过构造反例与理论分析，刻画MCR²在OOD场景下的失效模式，并检验引入IRM/REx不变性原理后的效果。

Result: 发现两重局限：其一，仅靠MCR²目标可达到全局编码最优却预测完全失败，即使存在完美稳定特征；其二，直接引入IRM/REx的不变性原理无法消除该失败，失败表示在各训练环境下具有相同最优编码算子。甚至当所有测试输入均可能在训练中出现时，编码质量可任意接近最优而预测误差任意接近100%。

Conclusion: MCR²的可靠OOD保证需要额外的新假设或学习原则来建立跨环境的稳定预测关系，共享编码最优性不足以确保预测稳定。

Abstract: Substantial efforts have been devoted to making deep learning objectives, representations, and architectures interpretable, with the goal of improving the safety, robustness, and generalisation of learning systems in diverse real-world applications. The recently proposed maximal coding rate reduction ($\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$) offers a promising information-theoretic framework for learning structured, discriminative representations of class-wise submanifolds and has inspired interpretable white-box architectures. However, we observe that $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ can completely fail under distribution shift, motivating our study of its out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation limits. We establish two limitations of $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ for OOD generalisation. First, the $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ objective alone can admit complete prediction failure: a representation based entirely on unstable environmental features can achieve the global coding optimum yet fail completely after correlation reversal, despite an available perfectly stable feature. This exact-optimum example includes test inputs that cannot occur during training. Even when every possible test input can also occur during training, coding quality can be arbitrarily close to optimal while prediction error is arbitrarily close to 100%. Second, directly incorporating the invariance principle underlying widely successful invariant risk minimisation (IRM) and risk extrapolation (REx) does not eliminate this failure. The failing representation admits the same optimal coding operator across training environments, showing that shared coding optimality does not ensure stable prediction. Reliable OOD guarantees for $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ therefore require additional new assumptions or learning principles that establish stable predictive relationships across environments.

</details>


### [203] [Scaling Discovery through Test-Time Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21032)
*Jongho Park,Vasilis Kontonis,Shivam Garg,Akshay Krishnamurthy,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，在测试阶段让多个智能体相互通信，可在困难任务上显著超越独立并行尝试，团队@k的成功率相当于4k个独立智能体，且优势随规模扩大而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学进步依赖协作，但现有智能体系统很少体现这一点；智能体之间通信是否有益仍是开放问题，先前结果好坏参半，因此作者系统研究测试时通信对多智能体问题求解的影响。

Method: 构建无预设角色、通过共享目录通信的多智能体测试时协作框架，并在需要新问题求解的ARC-AGI-3基准上考察通信智能体的规模扩展效应，同时将结论迁移到多联骨牌装箱、MNIST分类器压缩等研究型任务进行验证。

Result: k个通信智能体（team@k）的成功率可匹配4k个独立智能体，且优势随k增长而累积；单个智能体无法解决的任务团队能可靠解决。在多联骨牌装箱上超过best@k与先前最优；在MNIST压缩上，4个智能体产生1957字节、99.4%测试精度的分类器，优于已知人类最优和最佳单智能体结果。

Conclusion: 测试时通信的收益并非无条件：计算资源有限或缺乏明确进展度量时，独立智能体可能更优；但在计算充足且反馈清晰时，多智能体通信能持续带来更强结果。

Abstract: Science advances not in isolation but through collaboration, yet existing agentic systems capture little of this. Whether communicating agents help remains an open question with mixed prior results. We show that test-time communication can substantially outperform independent parallel attempts on challenging tasks, where sharing a breakthrough can push the whole group forward. We first study the effect of scaling multi-agent test-time communication, where agents have no predefined roles and communicate via a shared directory, on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark requiring novel problem solving. We find that a team of $k$ communicating agents, team@$k$, matches the success rate of $4k$ independent agents, and this advantage grows with $k$, suggesting gains compound with scale. The effect is not merely efficiency: a task that no single agent can solve, a team of agents can solve reliably. Furthermore, these gains transfer to research-oriented tasks, given sufficient compute. On polyomino packing, communicating agents outperform best@$k$ and exceed the prior best-known score. On MNIST classifier compression, communication surpasses the best-known human solution. A team of four agents produced a 1,957-byte classifier submission achieving 99.4% test accuracy, smaller than both the best-known human solution and the best single-agent result. These gains are not unconditional. Independent agents may outperform communication when compute is limited or when a clear measure of progress is absent. However, under sufficient compute and clear feedback, multi-agent communication consistently yields stronger results.

</details>


### [204] [Stiefel-AdamW: Geometry-Aware AdamW for Linear Factorization Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21039)
*Emanuele Zangrando,Marco Sutti,Francesco Tudisco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Stiefel-AdamW，一种针对线性因式分解模块（如 LoRA）的优化器，通过将其中一个因子约束在 Stiefel 流形上，在保留 AdamW 对角预条件能力的同时消除因子爆炸并提升稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习中广泛存在 $W=BA$ 形式的线性因式分解模块（LoRA 适配器、低秩压缩层、自注意力 QK 乘积等），这类模块的因式分解非唯一，会导致训练不稳定并限制可用学习率。然而现有方法通常用忽略其几何结构的标准欧氏优化器来训练，未充分利用其内在几何特性。

Method: 提出 Stiefel-AdamW：将其中一个因子矩阵约束在 Stiefel 流形上，另一个保持欧氏空间，从而把完整的 $\mathrm{GL}(\mathbb{R}^r)$ 规范对称性约化为紧致正交对称性，既排除因子爆炸又保留 AdamW 的坐标级对角预条件。矩估计在环境欧氏空间中进行，几何仅通过切空间投影与流形回缩引入，实现开销极小，并继承黎曼方法的稳定性与标准收敛保证。

Result: 在 GPT2、ViT、Mistral 7B 的 LoRA 风格微调，以及 GPT2 在 OpenWebText 上的完整预训练任务上验证，相较强基线取得一致提升，且相比 AdamW 几乎无额外计算成本。

Conclusion: Stiefel-AdamW 可作为 AdamW 的近似即插即用替代方案，适用于各类线性因式分解模块，在几乎不增加成本的前提下同时获得稳定性收益与收敛性保证，具有广泛的实用价值。

Abstract: A pervasive structural pattern in modern deep learning is the linear factorization block: a submodule of the form $W = BA$ in which two parameter matrices are multiplied directly, with no intervening nonlinearity. Such blocks appear in LoRA adapters, low-rank compressed layers, query-key products of self-attention, and share a common pathology: the factorization is non-unique, which can destabilize training and limit usable learning rates. Despite this, factorization blocks are typically optimized with standard Euclidean methods that ignore the underlying geometry. We introduce Stiefel-AdamW, a near drop-in replacement for AdamW for use wherever such blocks appear. By constraining one factor on the Stiefel manifold while leaving the other Euclidean, Stiefel-AdamW relaxes the full $\mathrm{GL}(\mathbb{R}^r)$ gauge symmetry to a compact orthogonal symmetry, ruling out factor blow-up while retaining the coordinate-wise diagonal preconditioning that gives AdamW its practical strength. Moment estimation is performed in the ambient Euclidean space, with geometry entering only through a tangent-space projection and a manifold retraction. The implementation overhead over AdamW is minimal, and we show that the resulting optimizer inherits both the stability benefits of Riemannian methods and standard convergence guarantees. We validate Stiefel-AdamW on LoRA-style fine-tuning of GPT2, ViT, and Mistral 7B and on full pretraining of GPT2 on OpenWebText, showing consistent improvements over strong baselines at essentially no additional cost over AdamW.

</details>


### [205] [A Lightweight Plug-in Gate for Transformer-Based Time-Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21044)
*Hongkai Zhuang,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预编码器协变量准入接口，并以轻量级表示级门控实现，作为TimeXer、iTransformer和PatchTST的即插即用模块在多个时序数据集上评估，结果显示门控具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协变量丰富的时间序列预测需要决定外部变量如何进入目标预测路径，而现有Transformer预测器通常直接构建协变量表示并送入编码器，缺少显式的准入阶段，因此本文关注预编码器协变量准入这一输入侧接口。

Method: 用轻量级表示级预编码器门控实现该接口，对表示单元赋予sigmoid分数，并研究带使用正则化的变体以惩罚平均准入；在零额外调参协议下，将其作为TimeXer、iTransformer和PatchTST的插件模块。

Result: 在ETTm1、ETTm2、Traffic、Energy和ILI数据集上开展了配对预测比较、门控位置消融、初始化消融、受控协变量准入分析以及基于VIF的PFI诊断案例研究；在所测设置中门控与对应基线具有竞争力，使用惩罚降低了平均准入分数，同时预测误差接近未加惩罚的TimeXer设置。

Conclusion: 预编码器协变量准入可作为有效的输入侧接口，轻量级门控在不显著损害预测性能的前提下具备竞争力，使用正则化可调节准入程度，为协变量处理提供了新的设计角度。

Abstract: Covariate-rich time-series forecasting requires deciding how external variables enter the target forecasting path. Existing Transformer-based forecasters usually build a covariate representation and pass it to the encoder without an explicit admission stage. This paper studies pre-encoder covariate admission as an input-side interface that regulates that representation immediately before encoder processing. We implement the interface with a lightweight representation-level pre-encoder gate that assigns sigmoid scores to representation units, and we also study a usage-regularized variant that penalizes average admission. The interface is evaluated as a plug-in module for TimeXer, Inverted Transformer (iTransformer), and Patch Time Series Transformer (PatchTST) under a zero-extra-tuning protocol, where each gated model inherits the corresponding baseline configuration. Experiments on the Electricity Transformer Temperature minute-level (ETTm1 and ETTm2) datasets, Traffic, Energy, and influenza-like illness (ILI) include paired forecasting comparisons, gate-placement ablation, initialization ablation, controlled covariate-admission analysis, and a variance inflation factor (VIF)-informed permutation feature importance (PFI) diagnostic case study. In the tested settings, the gate is competitive with the corresponding baselines, and the usage penalty reduces average admission scores while keeping forecasting errors close to the unpenalized TimeXer setting.

</details>


### [206] [FedeRage: Provably Convergent Agnostic Federated Learning under General Client Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21057)
*Herlock Rahimi,Dionysis Kalogerias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文刻画了在参与分布未知情形下FedAvg实际求解的优化问题，并提出风险规避扩展方法FedeRage，在非IID与随机参与条件下提升准确率、公平性和收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在非IID数据和随机客户端参与下性能下降。基于经典FedAvg的改进方法通常假设服务器已知客户端参与概率，但实际部署中这一假设很少成立。因此需要研究参与分布完全未知、可能高度倾斜且各轮规模可变时FedAvg的实际优化目标，并设计无需该先验信息的改进方法。

Method: 论文首先讨论并刻画了分布不可知（distributionally agnostic）FedAvg在参与情况完全未知时实际求解的优化问题：证明均匀聚合最小化一个由参与诱导边际加权的随机目标，并在凸且可能非光滑损失下达到标准O(1/√T)收敛率。基于此，提出Federated Risk-Averse Averaging（FedeRage），在自然分布鲁棒优化（DRO）框架下将条件风险价值（CVaR）嵌入本地目标，隐性提高高损失和低参与频率客户端的权重，且每客户端仅增加单个标量。方法在κ为风险规避“价格”上界时具有O(κ/√T)速率，并且不需要参与分布作为输入。

Result: FedeRage在三个异构基准实验上相比现有最先进方法在准确率、公平性和收敛速度方面均取得一致提升。理论分析给出了均匀聚合的O(1/√T)收敛率以及FedeRage的O(κ/√T)收敛率。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了参与分布未知时FedAvg的实际优化目标，并提出无需已知参与分布的FedeRage方法，通过CVaR和DRO实现风险规避式聚合，在理论和实验上均优于依赖分布信息或最优传输对齐的方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw data, but its performance degrades under non-IID data and stochastic client participation. Remedies built on classical Federated Averaging (FedAvg) typically presuppose that client participation probabilities are known to the server, which is rarely the case in deployed systems. We first discuss and then characterize the optimization problem that \emph{distributionally agnostic} FedAvg actually solves when participation is entirely unknown, possibly highly skewed, and of variable size across rounds: uniform aggregation is shown to minimize a well-defined stochastic objective, weighted by the participation-induced marginal, at a standard $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ rate for convex and possibly nonsmooth losses. Building on this characterization, we propose \emph{Federated Risk-Averse Averaging} (\textsc{FedeRage}), a risk-averse extension of FedAvg that embeds the \emph{Conditional Value-at-Risk} (CVaR) into the local objective within a natural distributionally robust optimization (DRO) framework. \textsc{FedeRage} implicitly upweights high-loss and infrequently participating clients while adding only a \emph{single scalar per-client}, and admits an $\mathcal{O}(κ/\sqrt{T})$ rate in which the factor $κ$ is the upper bound on the ``price" of risk aversion. In contrast with aggregation-alignment schemes based on optimal transport, which require the availability distribution as an input, \textsc{FedeRage} remains agnostic to it. Several experiments on three heterogeneous benchmarks indicate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods in accuracy, fairness, and convergence speed.

</details>


### [207] [Toward individual-level calibration in affect recognition with perceptual adjustment queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21073)
*Xuanzhou Chen,Sankaraleengam Alagapan,Ashwin Pananjady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于感知调整查询（PAQ）的框架，估计个体对面部情绪的恰可察觉差异（JND），以在感知空间中对任务难度进行等化校准，并在2AFC任务中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为任务通常假设相同刺激对所有被试造成等同的知觉难度，但个体在知觉敏感性上的差异系统性地违反了这一假设，因此需要一种个体化的知觉难度归一化方法。

Method: 以情绪知觉任务为测试平台，通过认知负荷较轻的感知调整查询（PAQ）直接估计每名被试在面部情绪谱上的恰可察觉差异（JND）；用PAQ推断的JND重新表达刺激距离，在感知空间中构造难度等化的任务。采用二选一强制选择（2AFC）任务，使用二元元认知难度判断与反应时方差分解两种互补行为指标进行验证，并与未校准基线及群体级Weibull校准作对比。

Result: PAQ校准在个体层面显著等化了主观感知到的任务难度，优于未校准基线和群体级Weibull校准，同时降低了平均反应时以及反应时的被试间方差。

Conclusion: PAQ是一种有原则且实用的工具，可用于面部情绪识别中的个体化知觉校准。

Abstract: Behavioral tasks measuring facial affect perception assume that identical stimuli impose equivalent perceptual difficulty across participants. However, this assumption is systematically violated by individual differences in perceptual sensitivity. Using an affective perception task as our testbed, we propose a framework to normalize for perceptual difficulty that directly estimates each participant's Just Noticeable Difference (JND) along the facial affect spectrum via cognitively lightweight perceptual adjustment queries (PAQs). We use these PAQ-inferred JNDs to re-express stimulus distances, constructing difficulty-equated tasks in perceptual space. We validate the framework in a Two-Alternative Forced-Choice (2AFC) task using two complementary behavioral measures: binary metacognitive difficulty judgments and response time variance decomposition. We find that PAQ calibration significantly equalizes perceived task difficulty at an individual level when compared to both the non-calibrated baseline and population-level Weibull calibration, while also reducing mean response time and between-subject variance in response time. These results establish PAQ as a principled and practical instrument for individualized perceptual calibration in facial affect recognition.

</details>


### [208] [REFINEPPO: Learning Continuous Control Policies by Iterative Action Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21108)
*Sachini Weerasekara,Sagar Kamarthi,Jacqueline Isaacs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代动作精炼方法IAR及REFINEPPO，通过残差校正逐步构建控制动作，在14个基准任务上匹配或超越标准PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在连续控制中表现优异，但策略通常是从状态到动作的单次前馈映射，一旦形成初始预测便缺乏重新考虑或逐步改进动作的机会。

Method: 提出IAR（迭代动作精炼）：从初始动作提案出发，由共享精炼网络反复结合状态与当前动作提案，通过一系列学习到的残差校正逐步改进动作，最终精炼结果用于执行。将IAR与PPO结合形成REFINEPPO。

Result: 在14个基准控制任务上评估REFINEPPO，并进行了精炼深度与更新调度的受控消融实验及有效性分析；结果显示其性能匹配或超越标准PPO，并在若干任务上收敛更快。

Conclusion: 迭代式动作构建是直接动作预测的有效替代方案，能为策略学习带来优势，且与现有PPO框架兼容。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has achieved strong performance across a wide range of continuous-control problems. These continuous-control policies, however, are often defined as direct mappings from an observed state to an action or action distribution, requiring a single feed-forward network to construct an optimal control decision in one pass. While effective, this formulation leaves little opportunity for the policy to reconsider or progressively improve an action once an initial prediction has been formed. In this work, we explore an alternative approach: rather than learning only to directly predict an action, can a policy learn to iteratively improve one, and can this iterative process provide advantages during policy learning? We introduce Iterative Action Refinement (IAR), an iterative action-construction method that constructs control actions through a sequence of learned residual corrections. Starting from an initial proposal, a shared refinement network repeatedly conditions on the observed state and the current action proposal, allowing each refinement step to revise the action constructed by preceding steps. The final refined proposal is then used to determine the action executed by the agent. We integrate this iterative action-construction mechanism with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), yielding REFINEPPO. We evaluate REFINEPPO across 14 benchmark control tasks, complemented by controlled ablations of refinement depth and update schedules and analyses aimed at understanding why iterative refinement is effective. Across these environments, REFINEPPO matches or exceeds the performance of standard PPO while demonstrating faster convergence on several tasks.

</details>


### [209] [Talk to Me, Jarvis: An Open-Source Edge-Deployable Voice Assistant Framework for Autonomous Racecars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21109)
*Daniel Henel,Frederik Werner,Alexander Langmann,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 开发 Jarvis 离线语音助手，基于 Mistral 7B 微调实现自动驾驶高层行为指令分类，意图识别准确率 97.63%，平均延迟 1.39 秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线托管的大语言模型存在网络依赖和推理延迟波动问题，不适合自动驾驶等时间关键型应用。

Method: 将语音识别与合成、自然语言指令分类集成到轻量级本地框架中，核心是经特定领域微调的 Mistral 7B 文本到指令分类器。

Result: 性能优于更大的在线托管模型，意图识别准确率 97.63%，平均处理延迟 1.39 秒，并提供开源实现。

Conclusion: 离线轻量级 LLM 方案可满足自动驾驶对快速响应的需求，并支持后续研究与微调。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have improved their effectiveness as back-end components for voice assistants, particularly in intent understanding and context-aware input classification. However, online-hosted models introduce network dependency and variable inference latency, limiting their suitability for time-critical autonomous driving applications. In this work, we address these issues by developing Jarvis, an offline voice assistant for high-level behavioral commands of autonomous vehicles. Its architecture integrates speech recognition and synthesis with natural language command classification into a lightweight, local framework. Jarvis core component is a text-to-command classifier, built using a domain-specific fine-tuning of the Mistral 7B model, demonstrating low-latency inference. Our experimental evaluation demonstrates that our solution outperforms larger online-hosted models, achieving 97.63 % intent recognition accuracy with an average processing latency of 1.39 s, making it well-suited for operations requiring quick response times. To support further research and fine-tuning, we provide an open-source implementation.

</details>


### [210] [Signal-Centric Remote Sensing via Alternative Preprocessing and Acoustic Processing for ML-Driven Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21123)
*Logan Luna,Sirio Jansen-Sánchez,Ilteris Demirkiran,Leo Ghelarducci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用CSV格式原始数据替代图像表示处理声纳数据，处理时间降低91.18%，机器学习目标检测精度提升，SNR、PSNR等指标改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流声纳数据处理依赖图像表示，需要在自主系统上对图像数据做预处理，计算开销大、效率低。

Method: 针对遥感应用提出一种以逗号分隔值（CSV）格式数据为输入的替代处理流程，绕过图像化预处理，并在该数据格式上直接开展机器学习实验。

Result: 处理时间减少91.18%；机器学习的目标检测准确率获得提升；信噪比（SNR）、峰值信噪比（PSNR）及其他评估指标均有提高。

Conclusion: 以CSV等非图像格式直接处理声纳数据，可在效率与检测质量上同时优于传统图像化方法，为遥感与自主系统应用提供更轻量的替代方案。

Abstract: The dominant method of processing sonar data is using image-based representations, requiring the preprocessing of image data on autonomous systems. We propose an alternative data processing method for remote sensing applications via the use of data in Comma-Seperated Value format. Experimentation on our alternative approach shows a reduction of processing time by 91.18%, an improvement in accurate object detection by Machine Learning, and an increase in SNR (Signal-to-noise ratio), PSNR (Peak signal-to-noise ratio), and other evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [211] [Layerwise Decoupling for Stable Structured Sparsification of Fully Connected Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21126)
*Charles Kulick,Armenak Petrosyan,Sui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种解耦的逐层结构化稀疏化方法，用于预训练神经网络全连接层的剪枝，理论证明其与联合惩罚等价，实验显示比耦合方法更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化稀疏化方法通常对所有层联合施加惩罚，容易在较大正则强度下出现灾难性过度剪枝，可用正则范围窄。作者希望找到一种更稳健的剪枝方式。

Method: 提取浅层双层子网络，对内层权重归一化，对外层权重矩阵施加结构化组惩罚，逐层顺序处理以剪枝神经元并缩减每层宽度。理论上证明该解耦约束目标在最优性上等价于对内外权重的特定联合惩罚（对任意正齐次激活函数成立），从而得到简洁的投影与近端公式。

Result: 解耦重构比耦合方法更稳健：可用正则强度范围更宽，灾难性过度剪枝率更低，同时保持可比精度。在受控分类与稀疏恢复实验中验证了这些性质，并在高维PINN压力测试和OPT-1.3B前馈层中考察了适用范围。

Conclusion: 解耦的逐层结构化稀疏化能在保持精度的同时提升剪枝稳健性，适用于从受控实验到实际大模型（OPT-1.3B）的场景。

Abstract: We propose a decoupled, layerwise method for structurally sparsifying the fully connected layers of pretrained neural networks. Rather than penalizing all layers jointly, our approach extracts shallow two-layer subnetworks, normalizes the inner weights, and applies a structured group penalty to the outer weight matrix of each block, processing layers sequentially to prune neurons and reduce the width of each layer. We prove that the constrained decoupled objective is equivalent at optimality to a specific joint penalty on the inner and outer weights, for any positively homogeneous activation, and thus admits a clean projected and proximal formulation. Our central finding is that this decoupled reformulation is more robust than coupled methods. In numerical experiments it provides a wider usable range of the regularization strength and a lower rate of catastrophic over-pruning than the tested joint baseline while maintaining comparable accuracy. We establish these properties in controlled classification and sparse-recovery studies, and examine their scope in a high-dimensional PINN stress test and in the feed-forward layers of OPT-1.3B.

</details>


### [212] [EnSol: an environment-aware graph neural network for molecular solubility prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21151)
*Thao Nguyen,Saman Shafaei,Zhengyi Zhang,Huimin Zhao,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EnSol 是一个环境感知的概率框架，将溶质-溶剂建模为分子图，用交叉注意力捕捉相互作用，并通过温度特征调制与混合密度网络预测完整溶解度分布，在多个基准和实验验证中超越现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子溶解度影响反应可行性、配方性能、分离效率和溶剂选择等关键环节。但实验测量成本高、覆盖稀疏；现有计算模型多依赖固定溶剂假设、确定性建模或简化的相互作用表示，难以刻画复杂分子间作用、连续温度效应和实验不确定性。

Method: EnSol 将溶质与溶剂分别表示为分子图并学习独立表征，再通过交叉注意力融合以捕捉溶质-溶剂相互作用；温度通过特征级调制直接注入溶剂环境表征；最后由混合密度网络输出完整溶解度分布，以刻画温度依赖行为与实验不确定性。

Result: 在独立的 SolProp 与 Leeds 基准数据集上，EnSol 分别取得 0.876 和 0.601 的 Spearman 相关系数，均优于当前最先进的溶解度预测模型。在化学多样性的溶质-溶剂对实验中，EnSol 保持较强预测性能并支持可靠的溶剂排序，Spearman 相关系数为 0.715。

Conclusion: 结果表明，EnSol 能在多种化学体系中实现可靠的溶解度预测与溶剂选择，同时给出预测不确定性，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Molecular solubility directly affects key aspects of molecular development such as reaction feasibility, formulation performance, separation efficiency, and solvent selection. However, experimental measurement across solutes, solvents, and temperatures remains costly and sparsely sampled. Existing computational models often rely on fixed-solvent assumptions, deterministic formulations, or simplified representations of solute-solvent interactions, limiting their ability to capture complex molecular interactions, continuous temperature effects, and experimental uncertainty. Here, we introduce EnSol, an environment-aware probabilistic framework for molecular solubility prediction. EnSol represents the solute and solvent as molecular graphs and learns separate representations for each before bringing them together through cross-attention to capture solute-solvent interactions. Temperature is incorporated directly into the solvent environment through feature-wise modulation, and a mixture density network predicts full solubility distributions to capture both temperature-dependent behavior and experimental uncertainty. On the independent SolProp and Leeds benchmark datasets, EnSol achieved Spearman correlations of 0.876 and 0.601, respectively, outperforming state-of-the-art solubility prediction models across both benchmarks. Beyond computational benchmarking, experimental validation across chemically diverse solute-solvent pairs showed that EnSol maintained strong predictive performance and supported reliable solvent ranking, achieving a Spearman correlation of 0.715. These results show that EnSol can support reliable solubility prediction and solvent selection across diverse chemical systems while accounting for predictive uncertainty.

</details>


### [213] [HMB-GAN: Hybrid Multi-Bézier GAN for Vector Shape Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21158)
*Elian Hugh Thiele-Evans,Binh Duong Pham,Hani Omar M Alharbi,Liibaan Aaden,Syed Umer Hasnain Zaidi,Prem Prakash Jayaraman,Muhammad Saeed,Boris Eisenbart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出HMB-GAN，一种混合量子-经典GAN框架，用于生成CAD可用的多段贝塞尔向量几何；量子生成器收敛更快、参数更少，但受限于硬件与模拟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式几何建模工作大多在栅格化或单段贝塞尔域中操作，难以直接生成符合CAD要求、具有几何连续性的闭合向量形状，因此需要新的生成框架。

Method: 提出HMB-GAN（Hybrid Multi-Bézier GAN），端到端可微的生成框架，通过拼接多段贝塞尔表示构造闭合形状，并在构造过程中强制几何连续性；在同一架构下对比量子增强生成器与经典生成器。

Result: 量子生成器收敛更快、模型参数量减少，在点云分布指标上略有提升；但存在严重的模拟器开销，经典模拟评估受硬件条件制约。

Conclusion: 结果证明了通过混合量子架构对结构化几何进行建模的可行性，同时凸显了当前量子硬件在实用性与开销方面的局限。

Abstract: We explore the use of hybrid quantum-classical generative adversarial networks for synthesising CAD-ready vector geometries. Unlike prior work that operates in rasterised or single-Bézier domains, we introduce HMB-GAN (Hybrid Multi-Bézier GAN), an end-to-end differentiable generative framework that constructs closed shapes through stitched multi-segment Bézier representations with geometric continuity enforced by construction. We compare a quantum-enhanced generator with a classical generator within this architecture and evaluate them across point cloud distribution metrics and geometric shape statistics. Results show that despite faster convergence, a reduction in model parameter count, and slightly improved performance on point cloud metrics, the quantum generator suffers from excessive simulator overhead and thus classically-simulated evaluation suffers from hardware constraints. These results demonstrate the feasibility of modelling structured geometries through hybrid quantum architectures whilst highlighting contemporary hardware limitations.

</details>


### [214] [M2G-LLM: Enhancing Clinical Prediction via Multimodal Graph Reasoning and LLM Context Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21164)
*Inyoung Choi,Sukwon Yun,Jiayi Xin,Jie Peng,Tianlong Chen,Qi Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出M2G-LLM框架，用图神经网络增强大语言模型的多模态医疗数据整合能力，在MIMIC数据集上提升临床预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策需要整合临床笔记、检验结果与医学影像等多种模态数据，而现有大语言模型虽擅长处理非结构化临床文本，却难以纳入非文本模态，限制了其在医疗场景中的应用广度。

Method: M2G-LLM（多模态医学图-LLM）通过图神经网络实现多模态整合与对齐：建模患者多次就诊之间的时间关系，在临床相似患者间传播信息，并对异质数据源进行对齐，构建增强的多模态上下文向量；这些向量被注入到大语言模型的中间层，从而实现文本与非文本模态的联合推理。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV与MIMIC-CXR数据集上，M2G-LLM在临床预测任务中相较强基线模型取得提升。

Conclusion: 结果显示了将大语言模型的语言理解能力与图神经网络的关系推理能力相结合，用于全面多模态医疗分析的潜力。

Abstract: Integrating diverse data modalities --- such as clinical notes, laboratory results, and medical imaging --- is essential for advancing clinical decision-making. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable performance in processing unstructured clinical text, their limited capacity to incorporate non-text modalities hinders their broader utility in healthcare applications.
