Here, we introduce M2G-LLM (Multimodal MedGraph-LLM), a novel framework that enhances LLMs with multimodal integration and alignment via Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). Our approach models temporal relationships between patient visits, propagates information across clinically similar patients, and aligns heterogeneous data sources to construct enriched multimodal context vectors. These vectors are injected into the intermediate layers of the LLM, enabling joint reasoning over textual and non-textual modalities. We evaluate M2G-LLM on the MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-CXR datasets, demonstrating improvements in clinical prediction tasks over strong baseline models. Our results highlight the promise of combining the language understanding of LLMs with the relational reasoning capabilities of GNNs for comprehensive, multimodal healthcare analysis.

</details>


### [215] [TierKV: Long-Context On-Device LLMs via Predictive Multi-Tier KV Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21172)
*Zhihao Shu,Md Musfiqur Rahman Sanim,Jie Hu,Kun Yuan,Minghai Qin,Gagan Agrawal,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TierKV 是一个面向移动端 LLM 推理的框架，通过预测式多层级 KV 缓存优化（PMCO），在解码前预测缓存需求并将 token 联合分配到精确、低秩和闪存卸载三层，在保持完整上下文访问的同时大幅降低内存占用并提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 正被部署到移动设备上处理文本、图像、视频和音频等多样的长上下文任务，而 KV 缓存随序列长度线性增长且在每步解码时都被访问，成为主要的内存瓶颈。现有方法（低秩压缩、token 驱逐、闪存卸载）分别存在重建开销大、token 不可逆丢失或 I/O 停顿等问题，节省内存的收益常被这些代价抵消。

Method: 提出 TierKV 推理框架，其核心是预测式多层级缓存优化（PMCO）。在解码开始前，PMCO 利用 prefill 阶段的隐藏状态预测未来的缓存需求，并在设备内存与精度预算约束下，将 token 联合分配到精确（exact）、低秩（low-rank）和闪存卸载（flash-offloaded）三个层级。该形式化保留了完整上下文的访问能力，消除了反应式驱逐的循环依赖，并给出了可在运行时选择层级边界和每层秩的闭式求解器。

Result: 在三款移动 SoC、涵盖文本、视觉和音频的八个模型上，TierKV 相比现有移动端 LLM 框架将 prefill 吞吐提升最高达 17.6 倍，常驻 RAM 的 KV 缓存减少 12.5%–34%，从而在相同内存预算下支持显著更长的上下文，且精度下降仅属轻微。

Conclusion: TierKV 通过在解码前进行预测式、多层级的 KV 缓存分配，兼顾了内存节省、吞吐提升与上下文完整性，为移动端长上下文 LLM 推理提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are moving onto mobile devices for increasingly diverse workloads over text, images, video, and audio. These applications often require long contexts, making the Key-Value (KV) cache a dominant memory bottleneck because it grows linearly with sequence length and is accessed at every decoding step. Prior work reduces KV-cache footprint through low-rank compression, token eviction, or flash offloading, but the resulting reconstruction overhead, irreversible token loss, or I/O stalls can offset the benefit of saving memory. We present TierKV, a mobile LLM inference framework built on Predictive Multi-Tier Cache Optimization (PMCO). Before decoding starts, PMCO predicts future cache demand from prefill hidden states and jointly assigns tokens to exact, low-rank, and flash-offloaded tiers under the device memory and accuracy budgets. This formulation retains access to the full context, removes the circular dependency of reactive eviction, and admits a closed-form solver that selects tier boundaries and per-layer ranks at runtime. Across eight text, vision, and audio models on three mobile SoCs, TierKV improves prefill throughput by up to 17.6x over existing mobile LLM frameworks, reduces RAM-resident KV cache by 12.5-34%, thereby enabling substantially longer contexts under the same memory budget, while incurring only minor accuracy degradation.

</details>


### [216] [SWE-Proof: Can Language Models Resolve Real-World Issues with Machine-Checked Proofs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21190)
*George Ma,Benjamin Mikek,Haoyu Li,Ferhat Erata,Yuhao Zhang,Zeren Shui,Behrooz Omidvar Tehrani,Jun Huan,Murali Krishna Ramanathan,Somayeh Sojoudi,Hao Zhou,Anoop Deoras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Benchproofer 流水线，把真实代码修复任务转化为可形式化验证的任务，构建 SWE-Proof（500 个形式化验证的真实 issue），发现测试通过的补丁中约 1/4 到 1/2 存在反例，而规格生成本身是核心难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体代码生成基准依赖留出测试集判定正确性，测试集天然不完整且易被记忆；形式化验证可规避这两点，但已有工作只覆盖规格作为输入给出的独立任务，无法处理涉及大型仓库、意图以模糊自然语言表述的真实 issue。

Method: 提出 Benchproofer 流水线：对已知正确补丁的编码任务撰写新代码的形式化规格，用公理概括被调用函数的行为，只有在机械门与对抗门一致通过后才接纳该实例。将其应用于 SWE-bench Verified 与 SWE-bench Pro，并对两个前沿模型进行评测，对比形式化规格、结构化自然语言规格与无辅助基线的效果。

Result: 构建了 SWE-Proof，含 500 个真实 issue，正确性经形式化验证。验证能捕捉测试遗漏的问题：1/4 至 1/2 的测试通过补丁存在反例，结构化自然语言规格无法解决该问题，而正确的形式化规格将 Opus 4.8 的解决率从 85% 提升至 95%。自行撰写规格的模型相比无辅助基线无提升，仅 62% 的规格通过审计，主要失败模式是忠实性不足（规格只约束部分必需行为、其余放任自由）。

Conclusion: 规格质量与最终结果高度相关：未解决实例中 89% 规格失败，已解决实例中仅 47% 失败。忠实规格的自动合成仍是一个具体且未解决的开放问题。

Abstract: Ensuring the correctness of LLM-generated code is a core challenge for modern software engineering. Benchmarks for agentic code generation check correctness with held-out test suites, which are inherently incomplete and increasingly susceptible to memorization. Formal verification avoids both problems, but existing work covers only standalone tasks whose specifications are given as input, not real issues, which touch large repositories and state intent in vague natural language. We present Benchproofer, a pipeline that turns a coding task with a known correct patch into a formally verified one: it writes a specification for the new code, summarizes the existing functions that code calls with axioms, and admits an instance only after mechanical and adversarial gates agree. Applying it to SWE-bench Verified yields SWE-Proof, 500 real issues whose correctness is formally verified rather than tested, and it extends to SWE-bench Pro. Across two frontier models, verification catches what tests miss: a quarter to a half of test-passing patches admit counterexamples, which a structured natural-language specification does not fix, while a correct formal one lifts resolution from 85% to 95% for Opus 4.8. Writing that specification is the hard part: models that must write their own gain nothing over an unaided baseline, and only 62% of their specifications pass our audit. The usual failure is faithfulness, a specification that constrains part of the required behavior and leaves the rest free. Specification quality still tracks the outcome, failing on 89% of unresolved instances against 47% of resolved ones, making faithful specification synthesis a concrete open problem.

</details>


### [217] [Reliability-Centered Evaluation of Sparse Longitudinal CT Lesion-Size Forecasting with Conformal Interval Calibration and Gompertz-Inspired Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21197)
*Lingfei Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于DeepLesion/DLT的五次随访同病灶轨迹基准（205条轨迹、129名患者），在固定预测视野下比较多种预测与不确定性方法，发现增加历史观测对预测精度提升有限，且精度、不确定性可靠性与轨迹一致性并不必然同步改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏的纵向CT随访限制了仅有少量历史观测时的病灶大小预测。作者希望厘清在控制预测视野后，增加历史观测是否真正带来预测收益，并系统评估点预测精度、不确定性可靠性与轨迹一致性三者的关系。

Method: 基于DeepLesion与Deep Lesion Tracker构建五次随访的同病灶轨迹基准（205条轨迹、129名患者）。采用固定访问索引视野设计，预测T3到T4的log变化，并逐步增加更早观测（m=1~4）。评估指标涵盖点预测精度、不确定性可靠性、事后保形区间校准、亚组表现，以及受Gompertz启发的轨迹正则化。

Result: 十个训练种子下平均留出RMSE在m=1、2、3、4时分别为0.4726、0.4305、0.4499、0.4513，m=2时最低，增加历史未改善RMSE。m=4时原始Cohort-Level Feature GP覆盖率接近95%名义水平，而MC Dropout、Deep Ensemble与残差尺度区间偏保守；患者级保形校准总体接近名义或偏保守，但区间更宽。患者分组交叉验证选出Gompertz项lambda*=0。全局群体参考常与病灶级变化方向相反，预测难度在解剖亚组间存在差异。

Conclusion: 在控制预测视野后，额外历史观测带来的预测收益有限；预测精度、不确定性可靠性与轨迹一致性并不必然共同提升，在稀疏纵向影像研究中应联合评估这三方面。

Abstract: Sparse longitudinal CT follow-up limits lesion-size forecasting when only a few prior observations are available. We constructed a five-visit DLT-derived same-lesion trajectory benchmark from DeepLesion and Deep Lesion Tracker (DLT), yielding 205 trajectories from 129 patients. We compared an exploratory conventional sparse-to-final analysis with a primary fixed visit-index horizon design predicting the common log change from T3 to T4 while progressively adding earlier observations, evaluating predictive accuracy, uncertainty reliability, post-hoc conformal interval calibration, subgroup performance, and Gompertz-inspired trajectory regularization. The evaluated methods showed partially overlapping point-prediction accuracy but distinct uncertainty behavior. Mean held-out RMSE across ten training seeds was 0.4726, 0.4305, 0.4499, and 0.4513 for m = 1, 2, 3, 4, indicating the lowest mean RMSE at m = 2; additional history did not improve RMSE. At m = 4, raw Cohort-Level Feature GP coverage was near the 95% nominal level, whereas MC Dropout, Deep Ensemble, and residual-scale intervals were conservative. Patient-level conformal calibration generally produced near-nominal or conservative coverage at the cost of wider intervals. Patient-grouped development cross-validation selected lambda* = 0 for the Gompertz-inspired term. A global population reference frequently opposed lesion-level change directions, and prediction difficulty varied across anatomical subgroups. Overall, additional historical observations provided limited predictive benefit once the prediction horizon was controlled, while predictive accuracy, uncertainty reliability, and trajectory consistency did not necessarily improve together, and should be evaluated jointly in sparse longitudinal imaging.

</details>


### [218] [MIRCID: Inferred Hub-miRNAs Drive Cross-Task Improvements in Drug Mechanistic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21280)
*Xin Cao,Yigang Chen,Jiatong Xu,Ziyue Zhang,Xiang Cheng,Shenyu Wang,Yangyi Zhang,Xiaoxuan Cai,Shidong Cui,Zihao Zhu,Xiang Ji,Hsi-Yuan Huang,Yang-Chi-Dung Lin,Hsien-Da Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MIRCID框架，用推断的枢纽miRNA（HubmiR）重编码转录组数据用于药物机制建模，miRNA增强效果优于TF活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物机制（MoA）建模通常依赖扰动转录组，但匹配的miRNA测量数据往往缺失，因此需要可扩展的方式复用现有数据。

Method: 构建MIRCID框架，将基因表达与推断的转录因子（TF）活性及miRNA表达进行比较，用于通路分类和基于相似度的MoA检索；其中HubmiRNet从977个L1000标志基因推断414个泛癌枢纽miRNA，并有1298输出的变体。

Result: HubmiRNet推断达到87.72%的Pearson相关系数；其1298输出变体在全miRNA任务上优于SiCmiR（71.21% vs 67.30%）；miRNA增强比TF活性带来更一致的增益；通用嵌入对照效用依赖模型；互补性分析发现一种独特、部分可线性恢复且保留基因来源结构的表示；案例显示弱转录信号样本中分类改善与合理的miRNA模式相关。

Conclusion: 推断的HubmiR可作为具有生物学依据的转录组重编码用于扰动药物建模，但实测扰动miRNA响应的恢复仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Drug mechanism-of-action (MoA) modeling commonly relies on perturbational transcriptomes, but matched microRNA (miRNA) measurements are often unavailable. Inferred regulatory features offer a scalable way to reuse these data. Here, we present MIRCID, a framework comparing gene expression with inferred transcription factor (TF) activity and miRNA expression across pathway classification and similarity-based MoA retrieval. HubmiRNet infers 414 pan-cancer hub miRNAs (HubmiRs) from 977 L1000 landmark genes, achieving a Pearson correlation coefficient of 87.72\%; its 1,298-output variant also outperformed SiCmiR on the full-miRNA task (71.21\% versus 67.30\%). In the evaluated comparisons, miRNA augmentation provided more consistent gains than TF activity. Generic embedding controls showed model-dependent utility, while complementarity analyses identified a distinct, partially linearly recoverable representation that retained gene-derived structure. Illustrative rescue cases linked improved classification to biologically plausible miRNA patterns in samples with weak transcriptional signatures. These findings support inferred HubmiRs as a biologically informed recoding of transcriptomic data for perturbational drug modeling, while leaving recovery of measured perturbational miRNA responses to further validation.

</details>


### [219] [Multi-Subject Pretraining Enables Short-Calibration Personalization for Closed-Corpus Surface EMG Speech Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21288)
*Chenqian Le,Beatrice Fumagalli,Yasamin Esmaeili,Xupeng Chen,Tianyu He,Nikasadat Emami,Adeen Flinker,Yao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在27名受试者、平均21.3分钟数据的少样本设定下，基于sEMG的默读语音接口通过单受试者检查点初始化+多人预训练+目标受试者微调，将50句封闭语料的CER降至21.7%，且3分钟校准即接近全量校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sEMG默读语音接口受跨用户差异大、校准负担重的限制，作者希望验证在数据极少（每人不足0.5小时）的情况下，能否通过检查点初始化与短时校准实现可行的个性化识别。

Method: 采用27名受试者、Aloud与Mimed两种语音、封闭50句语料，使用留一受试者评估；从公开的单受试者检查点初始化，在非留出受试者上预训练，再对目标受试者微调。对比无目标校准、直接检查点微调、随机初始化预训练等设置，并测试3分钟短校准与受试者专属适配器。

Result: 完整流程达21.7% CER与31.9% WER；无目标校准为49.3% CER，直接微调为68.0% CER；随机初始化预训练仅44.9% CER且27折中5折不收敛。预训练受试者从1人增至26人时宏平均CER由74.4%降至21.7%。3分钟校准为20.5% CER与31.7% WER，与约13分钟全量无显著差异；受试者专属适配器无收益。排除5句评估句后CER/WER升至78.6%/99.9%。

Conclusion: 在固定电极布局、封闭语料设定下，短时校准的个性化sEMG语音识别是可行的；检查点初始化和多受试者预训练是关键，而专属适配器与更多校准数据收益有限。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG)-based silent speech interfaces are limited by cross-user variability and calibration burden. We study a limited-data setting in which each of 27 speech-typical participants contributed less than 0.5 h of data (21.3 min on average) across Aloud and Mimed speech. Within a closed 50-sentence corpus, we used leave-one-subject-out evaluation, initializing from a released single-subject checkpoint, pretraining on non-held-out participants, and fine-tuning on the target participant. This pipeline achieved 21.7% character error rate (CER) and 31.9% word error rate (WER), compared with 49.3% CER without target-subject calibration and 68.0% CER for direct checkpoint fine-tuning. Multi-subject pretraining from random initialization followed by fine-tuning reached 44.9% CER and did not converge under the fixed schedule in 5 of 27 folds, indicating substantial optimization and accuracy benefits from checkpoint initialization. Macro-averaged CER declined from 74.4% with one pretraining participant to 21.7% with 26. Three minutes of target-subject calibration achieved 20.5% CER and 31.7% WER, with no statistically significant difference from the full approximately 13-min pool (21.7% CER and 31.9% WER). A subject-specific adapter provided no detectable benefit. Excluding the five evaluation sentences from all sEMG model-training data increased CER and WER to 78.6% and 99.9%. These results support short-calibration personalization in a standardized-montage, closed-corpus setting.

</details>


### [220] [FairLMs: A Turnkey Library for Fairness in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21296)
*Jiale Zhang,Michael Larionov,Zichong Wang,Zhipeng Yin,Wenbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairLMs：一个通过显式声明模型能力与输入需求来统一语言模型公平性测量、缓解与证据诊断的 Python 库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型公平性研究涉及偏差测量、缓解方法应用以及对评估所依赖证据的检查。现有工具功能互补但接口各异，整合时需协调模型接口、证据格式、访问限制和结果类型，导致适用性检查与方法比较困难。

Method: 提出 FairLMs 库，通过显式声明模型能力与输入需求来连接上述活动。提供 33 个内在与外在指标、涵盖四类干预的 14 个缓解组件、14 个数据集与评分工具诊断、适配三种 Transformer 架构及受支持托管补全 API 的适配器，以及基准加载器；声明在执行前被检查，结果附带获取时的配置。

Result: 实现了一个可组合、可比较、可扩展的工具库，使兼容组件能够组合、方法可在统一协议下比较、工作流可扩展到新模型与数据集；源代码已开源。

Conclusion: FairLMs 通过声明式设计降低了公平性工具链整合成本，为语言模型公平性研究提供了统一且可扩展的实验框架。

Abstract: Fairness research on language models involves measuring bias, applying mitigation methods, and examining the evidence on which an evaluation rests. Existing tools offer complementary functionality through different interfaces, so combining them requires reconciling model interfaces, evidence formats, access constraints, and result types before applicability can be checked or methods compared. We introduce \textbf{FairLMs}, a Python library that connects these activities through explicit declarations of model capabilities and input requirements. It provides 33 intrinsic and extrinsic metrics, 14 mitigation components spanning four intervention categories, 14 dataset and scoring-instrument diagnostics, adapters for the three Transformer architectures and supported hosted completion APIs, and benchmark loaders. Declarations are checked before execution and results carry the configuration under which they were obtained, so that compatible components can be combined, methods compared under a common protocol, and workflows extended to new models and datasets. The source code is available at: https://github.com/FairLMs/FairLMs.

</details>


### [221] [Fast And Accurate Text Content File Type Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21306)
*Manu Nandan,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于文本内容文件（尤其是源代码）类型识别的神经网络模型，在开源文件上平均准确率优于现有工具，速度约为Magika的4倍，体积小28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织中常需基于文件内容而非扩展名或magic number来识别文件类型，尤其在网络安全领域。现有方法存在不足：基于模型的工具（如Magika）在计算量和检测时间上有优化空间，而基于文件解析的工具在检测准确率上有欠缺。

Method: 提出一种神经网络模型，用于识别文本内容文件的类型，特别是源代码文件，以兼顾更高的准确率与更快的速度。

Result: 在开源文件上的实验表明，该模型在文本内容文件类型识别上的平均准确率更高，速度约比Magika快4倍，同时模型体积小28%。

Conclusion: 该神经网络模型在准确率、速度和体积上均优于现有工具，可为文件类型识别提供更高效、更轻量的解决方案。

Abstract: A common requirement across organizations is to have a tool that can identify file types based on their contents, particularly in the cybersecurity domain where magic numbers and file extensions can not be trusted. While existing tools work well in practice, there is plenty of room for improvement either in terms of computational load and time for detection in the case of model based tools like Magika or in terms of accuracy of detection in the case of file parsing tools that use programming language constructs. In this study, we propose a neural network model for identification of types of text content files, especially source code, that is more accurate and faster than other available tools. Our experiments on open-source files indicate that it is not only more accurate on average for text-content file-type identification, but also approximately four times faster than Magika, while being 28% smaller in size.

</details>


### [222] [An Introduction to Compression-Based Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21309)
*John Hurwitz,Edward Raff,Charles K. Nicholas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文综述并形式化了"压缩用于机器学习"的各种策略，提出并实证验证了一个基于压缩的机器学习设计框架，发现在部分任务上可与传统基线竞争，且在恶意软件检测上优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无损压缩算法（如gzip）可通过归一化压缩距离或最小描述长度原理转化为机器学习方法；自回归模型也可通过熵编码转化为无损压缩方法。这种看似循环的依赖关系在现代人工智能中具有尚未被充分发掘的潜力，因此需要系统梳理与形式化相关策略。

Method: 论文对利用压缩进行机器学习的各类策略进行了综述与形式化，并提出了一个面向"基于压缩的机器学习"的设计框架，通过实证方式进行验证。

Result: 基于压缩的方法与传统基线相比具有竞争力，在恶意软件检测任务上明显更强；改变这些设计选择可带来最高达 0.62 的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 压缩与机器学习之间存在深刻的双向联系，合理的框架设计能够显著提升基于压缩方法的性能，该方向在现代人工智能中具有重要研究价值与潜力。

Abstract: Any lossless compression algorithm (like gzip) may be converted into a machine learning method, via either Normalized Compression Distance or the Minimum Description Length principle. Any auto-regressive model may be converted into a lossless compression method via entropy coding. This seemingly circular dependence has unrealized potential in modern artificial intelligence and machine learning, and we survey and formalize the various strategies that have been used to leverage compression for machine learning. We introduce and empirically validate a design framework for compression-based ML, finding compression-based methods competitive with conventional baselines and decisively stronger on malware. We find that varying these design choices yields accuracy gains of up to 0.62.

</details>


### [223] [Deep Reinforcement Learning with Buffered Quantile Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21327)
*Mohammad Alipour-vaezi,Sajad Khodadadian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Deep-BQRL，一种无模型的分布式强化学习框架，将缓冲分位数学习扩展到神经函数逼近，用于风险敏感决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分位数强化学习通过优化累积回报分布的指定分位数实现可解释的风险敏感决策，但点分位数目标存在分位数在小扰动下突变、精确分位数规划计算代价高的问题。下缓冲分位数通过平均目标水平以下的相邻分位数缓解前者，保留点分位数目标的同时提供更平滑的替代；但现有方法仍基于模型并依赖显式回报律规划，局限于小型表格问题。

Method: Deep-BQRL为无模型分布式强化学习框架：直接从采样转移中学习条件回报分位数，从学得分位数函数的相关区域构造缓冲动作分数，并利用集成分歧引导探索。增强输入表示使学得策略能响应轨迹信息，而无需显式复现精确规划所需的分位数-状态递归。

Result: 在资产出售最优停止和滑溜FrozenLake实验中，与基于模型的UCB-BQRL及表格PPO、TRPO比较。资产出售中，Deep-BQRL在报告目标水平上取得比PPO和TRPO更小的平均累积点分位数策略差距，UCB-BQRL差距最小；学得的停止决策随目标分位数变化，体现可解释的风险敏感行为。

Conclusion: Deep-BQRL成功将缓冲分位数学习推广到神经函数逼近的无模型设定，在风险敏感最优停止任务中优于表格PPO/TRPO基线，并展示出随目标分位数变化的可解释决策行为。

Abstract: Quantile-based reinforcement learning provides an interpretable approach to risk-sensitive decision-making by optimizing a prescribed quantile of the cumulative-return distribution. Despite this appeal, learning under a point quantile objective is challenging: quantiles can change abruptly under small perturbations of the return distribution, and exact quantile-sensitive planning requires computationally demanding distributional optimization. Lower-buffered quantiles alleviate the former difficulty by averaging neighboring quantiles immediately below the target level, providing a smoother surrogate while preserving the underlying point-quantile objective. Existing methods based on this principle, however, remain model-based and rely on explicit return-law planning, limiting their applicability beyond small tabular problems. We develop Deep-BQRL, a model-free distributional reinforcement-learning framework that extends buffered-quantile learning to neural function approximation. The method learns conditional return quantiles directly from sampled transitions, constructs buffered action scores from the relevant region of the learned quantile function, and uses ensemble disagreement to guide exploration. An augmented input representation allows the learned policy to respond to trajectory information without explicitly reproducing the quantile-state recursion required by exact planning. Experiments on an asset-selling optimal-stopping problem and slippery FrozenLake compare Deep-BQRL with model-based UCB-BQRL and tabular PPO and TRPO implementations. In asset selling, Deep-BQRL attains smaller mean cumulative point-quantile policy gaps than PPO and TRPO at the reported target levels, while UCB-BQRL retains the smallest gaps. The learned stopping decisions also vary with the target quantile, providing an interpretable illustration of the method's risk-sensitive behavior.

</details>


### [224] [Routine Blood Tests Outperform CRP for Distinguishing Bacterial From Viral Infection in Children](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21332)
*Mihaela Demireva,Zhecho Mitev,Djuna Chinareva-Klimentova,Svetoslav Ivanov,Georgi Nalbantov,Dimitar Mitev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用906名2–14岁儿科患者的血常规(CBC)与CRP数据，构建监督分类模型区分病毒与细菌感染，XGBoost表现最佳（AUC 81.7%），优于仅基于CRP的决策规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急性感染是儿童就诊与住院的主要原因，但病毒与细菌感染在临床上难以区分，导致抗生素过度使用和耐药性加剧。研究旨在评估血常规(CBC)在判断当前感染类型方面的额外预测价值。

Method: 回顾性研究，纳入2022–2026年间906名2–14岁、病毒或细菌检测阳性的儿科患者，要求具备CBC结果与CRP测量值。以CBC参数、CRP水平及年龄作为输入特征，训练多种监督分类模型，并用AUC、敏感度和特异度评估性能。

Result: 表现最佳的模型为包含全部特征的XGBoost，样本外AUC为81.7%，敏感度70.8%，特异度79.2%。所有训练模型在AUC上均优于仅基于CRP的决策规则模型。

Conclusion: 抗生素处方决策应基于多种因素，包括但不限于CBC，其中部分因素目前尚未纳入常规临床实践。

Abstract: Acute infectious diseases are among the leading causes of medical consultations and hospitalizations in children worldwide. These infections are predominantly caused by viruses or bacteria, yet differentiating between the two remains a common clinical challenge. As a result, pediatricians often default to the safer option of prescribing antibiotics contributing to the growing problem of antimicrobial resistance. The objective is to assess the additional predictive value of CBC towards determining the current infection. This retrospective study used data from 906 pediatric patients aged between 2 and 14 years who were tested positive either for viral or bacterial infection between 2022 and 2026. Inclusion criteria further required availability of CBC results and CRP level measurements. These laboratory parameters as well as age were used as input features for several supervised classification models. Model performance was evaluated using AUC, sensitivity and specificity. The best performing model is XGBoost, which included all features, achieving out of-sample performance of AUC of 81.7% and sensitivity of 70.8%, specificity of 79.2%. All trained models outperform a CRP-based only decision-rule model in terms of AUC. We suggest that the decision to prescribe antibiotics should be based on a number of factors, including but not limited to CBC, some of which are not currently incorporated into routine practice.

</details>


### [225] [IntBMoE: Integrating Block-Level Conditioning into Expert Composition for Full-Participation Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21346)
*Ran Cheng,Longfei Xu,Zheng Liu,Kaikui Liu,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 IntBMoE，一种块条件化的 MoE 架构，通过"稠密专家组合 + 稀疏块执行"解耦参与度、执行量与物化量三个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MoE 设计无法独立设定三个量：单 token 的参与度（贡献知识的专家数）、执行量（实际计算的专家数=计算成本）、物化量（需构建存储的专家参数集数=内存成本）。稀疏路由降低执行与物化但牺牲参与度；稠密输出混合恢复参与度但执行随专家数增长；参数合并保持执行量为 1 但物化随路由决策数增长。

Method: IntBMoE 将稠密专家组合与稀疏块执行配对。块来自小型学习码本，每个条目对应一个块。每内部层由轻量超网络将该层专家池中所有专家基合并为一个组合专家，从而实现全参与；路由器仅将每个 token 送入少数块以保持稀疏执行；物化量由码本（而非输入）固定上界。另设计双路径残差门控（DPRG），通过乘法门控耦合两条独立组合路径。

Result: 在图像分类任务上持续优于代表性稀疏与稠密 MoE 基线；在语言建模与序列推荐任务上验证了跨视觉的泛化能力；已全量部署于高德生成式推荐系统，在 60ms 延迟预算下服务数亿用户，在线 A/B 测试取得 2.4% 相对 UVCTR 提升。

Conclusion: IntBMoE 通过块条件化设计首次解耦 MoE 的参与度、执行量与物化量，兼顾表达力与效率，并在大规模工业推荐场景中验证了有效性与实用性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) scales capacity, but existing designs cannot set three quantities independently. For a single token, participation is how many experts contribute knowledge to its output, execution is how many are actually computed (compute cost), and materialization is how many expert-sized parameter sets must be built and stored (memory cost). Sparse routing keeps execution and materialization low, but shrinks participation: for each token, only a few experts contribute. Dense output-mixing restores full participation, but its execution grows with the number of experts. Parameter-merging keeps execution at one expert, but its materialization grows with the number of routing decisions. We propose IntBMoE, a block-conditioned MoE that decouples all three by pairing dense expert composition with sparse block execution. Its blocks come from a small learned codebook, one per entry. At each internal layer, a lightweight hypernetwork merges all expert bases in that layer's pool into one composed expert. Participation is full, because every composed expert draws on the entire pool. Execution stays sparse, because a router sends each token to only a few blocks. Materialization is bounded, because the codebook, not the input, fixes how many blocks exist. Dual-Path Residual Gating (DPRG) further couples two independently composed paths through multiplicative gating. Experiments on image classification show consistent gains over representative sparse and dense MoE baselines. Additional experiments on language modeling and sequential recommendation validate its generalization beyond vision. IntBMoE is fully deployed in AMap's generative recommendation system, serving hundreds of millions of users under a 60ms latency budget, with a 2.4% relative UVCTR gain in online A/B testing. Our code is available at https://github.com/AMAP-ML/DreamX-Rec/.

</details>


### [226] [Knowledge-Graph-Augmented Chronos-2 for HEC-RAS Surrogate Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21381)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将冻结的时序基础模型Chronos-2与水利工程知识耦合（KG-Chronos-2），可显著提升HEC-RAS水位代理预报精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望探究将时序基础模型与水利工程领域知识相结合，能否改善对HEC-RAS水面高程（WSE）的代理预报性能，从而在固定基准上实现热启动预报。

Method: 提出KG-Chronos-2，将冻结的Chronos-2预测器与精确状态残差解码、图条件历史检索以及输入对齐校正相结合；使用2008年模拟进行任务特定拟合，并与持续性模型、残差LSTM、项目条件循环GeoFNO、水利DCRNN风格模型及冻结Chronos-2进行对比。评估覆盖2011与2002年模拟中64个固定24小时窗口，共享几何下71个河段、4675个断面。

Result: KG-Chronos-2达到事件均衡RMSE 0.246970（原生WSE单位），相对冻结Chronos-2降低14.13%，相对水利DCRNN风格模型降低29.38%，相对循环GeoFNO降低39.54%；与冻结Chronos-2的RMSE差异95%层次自举区间为[-0.075177, -0.016317]，并在六个完整系统中取得最低的活动窗口与末端预报RMSE。

Conclusion: 结果支持将冻结的时序预测器与工程知识耦合，用于固定基准上的HEC-RAS热启动预报。

Abstract: We investigate whether coupling a time-series foundation model to hydraulic project knowledge improves surrogate forecasting of HEC-RAS water-surface elevation (WSE). We present KG-Chronos-2, which combines a frozen Chronos-2 predictor with exact-state residual decoding, graph-conditioned historical retrieval, and input-aligned correction. We compare the method with persistence, a residual LSTM, project-conditioned recurrent GeoFNO, a hydraulic DCRNN-style model, and frozen Chronos-2. Task-specific fitting uses the 2008 simulation. Evaluation covers 64 fixed 24-hour windows from the 2011 and 2002 simulations at 4,675 cross sections in 71 reaches on a shared geometry. KG-Chronos-2 achieves event-balanced root-mean-square error 0.246970 in native WSE units. It reduces RMSE by 14.13% relative to frozen Chronos-2, 29.38% relative to the hydraulic DCRNN-style model, and 39.54% relative to recurrent GeoFNO. The 95% hierarchical-bootstrap interval for its event-balanced RMSE difference from frozen Chronos-2 is [-0.075177, -0.016317]. KG-Chronos-2 also achieves the lowest active-window and final-lead RMSE among the six completed systems. These results support coupling a frozen temporal predictor to project knowledge for warm-start HEC-RAS forecasting on the fixed benchmark.

</details>


### [227] [Probabilistic Forecasting of Business Process Executions with Neural Temporal Point Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21382)
*Jiaxin Yuan,Daniela Grigori,Han van der Aa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将下一活动与剩余时间预测建模为标记时间点过程的生成式序列建模，显式处理时间戳并列，用Transformer编码器+混合解码器以精确对数似然训练，在十个公开日志上点精度持平基线、分布校准与锐度更优且推理最省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务型系统的运营者依赖对运行中执行流程将如何继续的预测，而这类预测只有在其可靠性已知时才可据以行动。主流深度学习模型是判别式、确定性的：只输出单一的下一个活动与单一剩余时间估计，没有可供推理的分布。作者因此希望直接给出预测分布，以提供可靠性信息。

Method: 把问题转化为带标记时间点过程的生成式序列建模，该过程对下一个标记及其事件间隔时间定义联合密度，从而天然给出预测分布。针对真实事件日志中连续事件常具有相同时间戳、违反简单点过程假设的问题，显式处理这种并列（tie）。模型结构为Transformer编码器结合事件间隔时间上的混合（mixture）解码器，用精确对数似然进行训练。

Result: 在十个公开日志上，所得模型在点预测精度上与判别式基线相当，在剩余时间分布的校准度和锐度上优于基线，并且推理成本最低，因为一次前向传播即可获得完整预测分布，无需采样。

Conclusion: 生成式标记时间点过程建模可作为流程预测的有效替代方案：在不牺牲点精度的前提下提供更可靠的分布式预测，同时计算更高效。

Abstract: Operators of service-based systems act on forecasts of how a running execution will continue, and such a forecast is actionable only if its reliability is known. Mainstream deep-learning models for this task are discriminative and deterministic: they emit a single next activity and a single remaining-time estimate, without a distribution to reason over. We instead cast the problem as generative sequence modelling with marked temporal point processes, which define a joint density over the next mark and its inter-event time and therefore deliver predictive distributions by construction. Real event logs violate the simple-point-process assumption these models rest on, since consecutive events frequently carry identical timestamps; we handle such ties explicitly and combine a transformer encoder with a mixture decoder over inter-event times, trained by exact log-likelihood. On ten public logs, the resulting model matches discriminative baselines on point accuracy, dominates them on the calibration and sharpness of remaining-time distributions, and is the cheapest at inference, since a full predictive distribution is obtained in a single forward pass without sampling.

</details>


### [228] [Tracing the Evidence Behind Zero-Shot Time-Series Forecasting: A Source-First Taxonomy and Audit Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21425)
*Delun Kong,Wanyun Ling,Chenxi Liu,Ziyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张零样本时间序列预测应被理解为"证据访问声明"，并提出以证据来源为先的分类框架与审计议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本TSF定义仅强调"无需针对目标任务更新参数"，但这一条件无法区分系统实际依赖何种可迁移证据（如冻结LLM先验、时序预训练参数、检索外部记忆），导致基准比较含义不明。

Method: 提出"来源优先"分类法，将三类主要证据来源（冻结LLM先验复用、参数化时序预训练、检索增强外部记忆）与实现架构分离；并在确定来源后，补充四个审计问题：任务接口、预测对象与评分方式、预测时上下文、资源预算。

Result: 形成一套概念框架与审计问题清单，用于识别零样本TSF中不同系统的证据边界与接口假设（本文为立场/议程类工作，未报告实验数据）。

Conclusion: 零样本TSF排行榜应同时报告证据边界与接口假设，使基准进展真正反映可迁移的预测能力，而非未披露的上下文、记忆或预算变化。

Abstract: Zero-shot time-series forecasting (TSF) is often described as forecasting without target-specific parameter updates, but that training-status condition does not specify what evidence the system may use. A frozen language model prompted with serialized values, a time-series model pretrained on broad forecasting corpora, and a retrieval-augmented forecaster may all satisfy the no-update condition while drawing on different transferable evidence. This paper argues that zero-shot TSF should therefore be governed as an evidence-access claim. We propose a source-first taxonomy that separates three primary evidence sources---frozen LLM prior reuse, parametric time-series pretraining, and retrieval-augmented external memory---from the architectures that implement them. After the source is identified, four additional audit questions remain: task interface, forecast object and scoring, prediction-time context, and resource budget. The resulting agenda is to make zero-shot leaderboards auditable by reporting evidence boundaries and interface assumptions alongside scores, so that benchmark progress reflects transferable forecasting capability rather than undisclosed changes in context, memory, or budget.

</details>


### [229] [Decision-Focused Learning for Mean-Variance Portfolio Optimization via KKT-Based Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21427)
*Kensei Nosaka,Shunnosuke Ikeda,Yuichi Takano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种单层优化框架，将均值-方差投资组合优化的KKT最优性条件嵌入上层的决策聚焦学习过程中，在保留预算与卖空约束的同时避免代理损失或约束松弛带来的结构性错配，并在真实ETF数据上取得多指标最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两阶段方法先预测预期收益再求解优化问题，其训练目标是最小化预测误差，与下游投资组合决策质量不一致。决策聚焦学习（DFL）虽直接最小化决策损失，但现有MVO的DFL方法依赖代理损失或约束松弛以求可解，导致训练时的预测模型与评估时求解的带约束MVO之间存在结构性错配。

Method: 提出单层优化（single-level optimization）形式化方法，将下层均值-方差优化问题的Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) 最优性条件融入上层学习问题。该形式化显式保留预算约束和卖空约束，同时对标准非线性优化求解器保持可解性。方法还引入了正则化项。

Result: 在两个具有不同相关结构的资产池上，使用真实世界ETF数据进行滚动窗口实验。结果表明，该方法在多个投资指标上取得最佳性能，并且所提出的正则化带来了性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将KKT条件嵌入学习过程，所提单层框架在保持约束严格性的同时实现了端到端的决策聚焦训练，缓解了预测与决策之间的错配问题，在实证投资指标上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Mean-variance portfolio optimization (MVO) is a central framework in data-driven asset management. A widely adopted approach is a two-stage framework that first predicts expected returns and then solves the optimization problem based on these predictions, with the predictive models trained by minimizing prediction errors. However, this objective of prediction is not aligned with the quality of the downstream portfolio decision. Decision-focused learning (DFL), which directly minimizes the downstream decision loss within the learning process, has thus emerged as a promising direction. However, existing DFL approaches to MVO rely on surrogate losses or constraint relaxations for tractability, creating a structural mismatch between predictive model training and the constrained MVO solved at evaluation. We propose a single-level optimization formulation that incorporates the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) optimality conditions of the lower-level MVO into the upper-level learning problem. This formulation explicitly preserves the budget and short-sale constraints while remaining tractable for standard nonlinear optimization solvers. Rolling-window experiments on real-world ETF (Exchange Traded Funds) data across two asset universes with different correlation structures show that our method achieved the best performance on multiple investment metrics and also demonstrated performance improvement due to the proposed regularization.

</details>


### [230] [Optimal Randomized Proper Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21445)
*Zachary Chase,Idan Mehalel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了随机适当在线学习算法的最优期望错误界为 O(L(H) log T)，改进了此前 O(L(H) log^6 T) 的结果，并在最坏情况下达到常数意义下的最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习中，随机适当学习算法的最优错误界长期存在差距：此前最优结果为 O(L(H) log^6 T)，而最优下界暗示应为 O(L(H) log T)。本文旨在弥合这一差距，确定该问题的紧界。

Method: 作者针对函数类 H 的在线学习问题，分析随机适当学习算法，结合 Littlestone 维数 L(H) 与时间范围 T，构造并证明期望错误界的上界。

Result: 证明了最优期望错误界为 O(L(H) log T)，优于 Daskalakis 和 Golowich (STOC 2022) 的 O(L(H) log^6 T)。

Conclusion: 对于最坏情况下的函数类，该结果在通用常数意义下达到最优，从而确定了随机适当在线学习错误界的紧界。

Abstract: We prove that the optimal expected mistake bound of online learning a function class $\mathcal{H}$ by a randomized proper learning algorithm is $O(\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H}) \log T)$, where $\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H})$ is the Littlestone dimension of $\mathcal{H}$ and $T$ is the time horizon. Our result improves upon the previously best known bound of $O(\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H}) \log^6 T)$ given by Daskalakis and Golowich (STOC 2022), and is optimal up to a universal constant for worst-case classes.

</details>


### [231] [Understanding LLM Quantization through Activation-Guided Compensation and Orthogonal Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21450)
*Yamato Narita,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将局部权值-激活量化误差精确分解为"激活引导的权重补偿项"与"正交残差项"，并用持久通道离群量与规则激活量界定残差上界，从而推导出随机Hadamard旋转、符号选择与通道缩放的设计准则，在8个Llama与Mistral模型上以无反向传播配置取得了与梯度训练的SpinQuant相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练后权值-激活量化可降低显存与推理开销，但W4A4这类激进量化因激活离群值而难以实现，有效量化分辨率被破坏。已有的权重优化、通道缩放、正交旋转等方法虽能缓解问题，但各自针对哪些误差成分、彼此关系如何并不清楚，缺乏统一的理论解释与设计指导。

Method: 作者对局部权值-激活量化误差进行精确分解，拆为激活引导的权重补偿项与正交残差项；利用持久性的通道离群量与规则激活量对残差建立上界，据此判断哪些误差可由权重补偿解决、哪些必须依赖变换设计。随后基于残差界推导随机Hadamard旋转、符号选择与通道缩放的实践准则，解释随机符号如何抑制持久离群通道间的相长干涉、多符号模式采样如何改善变换选择，以及二阶矩均衡如何导出L2缩放规则、进一步松弛后如何还原SmoothQuant式L∞缩放。方法采用无反向传播的配置，在8个Llama与Mistral模型上进行评估。

Result: 在8个Llama与Mistral模型上的无反向传播配置实验中，所提准则达到了与需梯度训练的SpinQuant相当的性能，验证了误差分解与残差界推导出的设计准则的有效性。

Conclusion: 该误差分解为理解权值补偿与变换设计的分工提供了理论框架，并据此给出可操作的旋转、符号与缩放准则；无需梯度训练即可在W4A4量化下取得与SpinQuant竞争的表现，说明理论分析可有效指导高效量化方案设计。

Abstract: Post-training weight-activation quantization reduces the memory and inference costs of large language models, but aggressive W4A4 quantization remains difficult because activation outliers degrade effective quantization resolution. Although weight optimization, channel-wise scaling, and orthogonal rotation mitigate this problem, the error components they address and their relationship remain unclear. Using an exact decomposition of local weight-activation quantization error into an activation-guided weight compensation term and an orthogonal residual, we bound the residual using persistent channel-wise outlier and regular activation quantities. This decomposition clarifies which error components can be addressed by weight compensation and which require transformation design. We then use the residual bounds to derive practical guidelines for applying randomized Hadamard rotation, sign selection, and channel scaling. In particular, the analysis explains how random signs suppress constructive interference among persistent outlier channels, how sampling multiple sign patterns can improve transformation selection, and how second-moment balancing leads to an $L_2$ scaling rule while a further relaxation recovers SmoothQuant-style $L_\infty$ scaling. We evaluate these guidelines through backpropagation-free configurations across eight Llama and Mistral models, obtaining performance competitive with gradient-trained SpinQuant.

</details>


### [232] [Efficient Architecture Search under Leave-One-Subject-Out Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21457)
*Heinke Hihn,Friedhelm Schwenker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PainNAS：一种基于块的、受控泄漏的NAS方法，将LOSO评估中架构搜索次数从N降至B，在BioVid数据集上以更少参数和FLOPs取得相当准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化疼痛评估中深度网络架构设计主要依赖人工。将NAS嵌入LOSO评估时，完全嵌套实现需N次独立搜索，训练成本近似线性时计算量按O(N²)增长，代价高昂。

Method: 提出基于块的、控制数据泄漏的方法，在受试者之间共享NAS搜索过程，把搜索次数由N减少到B（B远小于N），称为PainNAS。

Result: 在BioVid Heat Pain数据集上，PainNAS达到与基线相当的被试级准确率，同时参数量和FLOPs显著更少。

Conclusion: 通过在受试者间共享NAS搜索，可大幅降低LOSO评估的计算成本而不牺牲准确率，为自动化疼痛评估提供了高效的架构搜索方案。

Abstract: Deep neural architectures are widely used for signal processing in automated pain assessment systems. However, architecture design has remained largely a manual task despite the potential efficiency benefits of Neural Architecture Search (NAS). Embedding NAS in a Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) evaluation is computationally demanding because a fully nested implementation requires $N$ independent architecture searches and, assuming approximately linear training cost, scales as $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$. We propose a block-based, leakage-controlled approach that shares NAS runs between subjects, reducing the number of searches from $N$ to $B$, where $B \ll N$, dubbed PainNAS. On the BioVid Heat Pain dataset, PainNAS yields comparable subject-level accuracy with substantially fewer parameters and FLOPs.

</details>


### [233] [What Must Survive? Exact Task-Information--State Frontiers for Resource-Sufficient Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21523)
*Ronald Katende*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在下游任务未知时系统压缩所需保留状态量，给出基于任务信息字母表大小的精确前沿公式，并分析其计算复杂度与应用示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统常在下游任务完全确定之前就被压缩，因此需要明确：在仅有有限任务预信息的情况下，最少需保留多少状态，以及预信息能节省多少状态。

Method: 针对有限线性任务族，假设任务消息先于状态形成、精确任务后揭示。以大小为 K 的建议字母表刻画精确前沿 p*(K)，即对所有不超过 K 个块的划分取最小、对每块联合任务算子取最大秩；b 比特前沿通过 K=min(2^b,|U|) 得到。进一步给出近似奇异值前沿、公共核心下界、精确直和律，并证明寻找最优建议划分的强 NP 难性及固定近似容差下的持续性。

Result: 给出精确与近似状态前沿理论及复杂度结论；示例包括：良态 softmax 注意力构造在 9 比特区分 512 种延续之一时实现 524,288→1,024 坐标前沿；域分解数字孪生得到界面加局部状态律及异构区域加权划分问题；分层多任务模型三比特将所需状态从 3136 降至 448 坐标，更多任务信息趋近不可约的 328 坐标单任务下界。

Conclusion: 提前任务信息可通过将任务空间划分为低秩联合任务算子的块来显著减少所需保留状态；最优划分一般难解，即使允许固定近似容差仍为 NP 难，但该框架能统一刻画注意力、数字孪生和多任务模型中的状态压缩前沿。

Abstract: A system may be compressed before its downstream task is fully known. We ask how much retained state is then necessary and how much can be saved by limited advance task information.
  For a finite family of linear tasks, a task message is revealed before state formation and the exact task only afterwards. For an advice alphabet of size $K$, the exact frontier is \[ p^*(K)= \min_{\substack{\Pcal\text{ partition of }\U\\|\Pcal|\le K}} \max_{C\in\Pcal}\rank(T_C), \] with the $b$-bit frontier obtained by setting $K=\min(2^b,|\U|)$. Thus advance task information reduces state through partitions whose joint task operators have low rank.
  We also give an approximate singular-value frontier, a common-core lower bound and exact direct-sum law, and strong NP-hardness of finding an optimal advice partition. The hardness persists at every fixed positive approximation tolerance.
  Three examples illustrate the result. A well-conditioned softmax attention construction gives an exact $524{,}288\to1{,}024$ coordinate frontier when nine bits resolve one of $512$ continuations. A domain-decomposed digital twin yields an interface-plus-local-state law and a weighted partition problem for heterogeneous regions.
  A hierarchical multi-task model gives a two-stage frontier in which three bits reduce the required state from $3136$ to $448$ coordinates, with further task information approaching the irreducible $328$-coordinate single-task floor.

</details>


### [234] [IncentRL: The Trade-Off Between Preference Guidance and Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21525)
*Xuening Wu,Yanlan Kang,Shenqin Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IncentRL通过在外在任务奖励中加入偏好的KL惩罚来引导强化学习，并理论刻画该引导对外在任务性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于偏好的奖励塑形可引导强化学习，但加入偏好信号可能意外改变原本要优化的任务；需要明确偏好引导对外在任务性能的影响边界。

Method: 在指定结果分布与偏好分布之间加入KL惩罚；针对有限折扣MDP与有界塑形代价，推导外在价值扰动界、保持原最优策略的严格动作间隙充分条件，并刻画大权重情形下的折扣累积偏好代价；实现上使用手工设计的距离型结果代理、固定偏好分布与分数加权系数搜索。

Result: 在MiniGrid DoorKey-8x8上，系数0.01时三随机种子两百万步后平均成功率98%，零系数基线为90.5%，且搜索逐渐偏向更小系数；实验仍为描述性，未隔离KL塑形与更简单替代方案的区别。

Conclusion: 该工作为偏好型强化学习中“利用引导改善学习而不过度扭曲原任务目标”这一核心权衡提供了有原则的分析视角。

Abstract: Preference-based reward shaping can guide reinforcement learning, but adding preference signals to the reward may unintentionally change the task being optimized. We address this problem with IncentRL, a framework that introduces preference guidance while explicitly characterizing its effect on external-task performance. IncentRL adds a Kullback--Leibler (KL) penalty between a specified outcome distribution and a preferred distribution. For finite discounted Markov decision processes with bounded shaping costs, we derive an external-value perturbation bound, establish a sufficient strict-action-gap condition for preserving the original optimal policy, and characterize the large-weight regime through discounted cumulative preference cost. Exact examples clarify the limits of these guarantees, including tied optima and support mismatch. We study a practical implementation using a hand-designed, distance-based outcome proxy, a fixed preference distribution, and score-weighted coefficient search. On MiniGrid DoorKey-8x8, the reported three-seed mean success rate after two million training steps reaches 98\% with coefficient 0.01, compared with 90.5\% for the reported zero-coefficient baseline, while the search progressively shifts toward smaller coefficients. Together, these results provide a principled view of the central trade-off in preference-based RL: using additional guidance to improve learning without excessively distorting the original task objective. The current experiments remain descriptive and do not yet isolate KL shaping from simpler alternatives.

</details>


### [235] [OpenMAS-GCom. A Diagnostic Benchmark for Graph-enhanced Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21527)
*Kairui Yang,Xunkai Li,Kaixiang Zhang,Minghao An,Zekai Chen,Yuxuan Ba,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OpenMAS-GCom 基准，用受控干预诊断图增强多智能体系统中通信结构、角色分配与信息流对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: G-MAS 通过通信图和角色分配协调 LLM 智能体，但最终得分比较混合了模型、通信模式、角色和计算成本差异，难以将性能差异归因到具体组件，存在评估归因问题。

Method: 将系统表示为协作单元、通信链接、共享中间信息与执行规则；通过固定任务、模型、提示与预算，只改动单一组件进行对比：重连通信边、移除专家或批评者智能体、替换中间消息为错误内容、执行中禁用工作智能体；在 29 个数据集 6 个领域上评估 17 种配置，并新增 400 个 G-MAS-Complex 任务。

Result: 移除专家智能体的平均损失大于移除批评者；在原始得分相近时，错误消息与工作智能体失效导致不同程度的性能退化；G-MAS-Complex 上最高准确率与最高每 token 准确率由不同配置取得。

Conclusion: OpenMAS-GCom 通过受控干预实现组件级归因，揭示角色、信息流与通信结构的独立影响，为 G-MAS 设计提供可诊断的评估范式。

Abstract: Graph-enhanced multi-agent systems (G-MAS) coordinate large language model agents through communication graphs and role assignments, which determine how agents exchange information and divide responsibilities. However, final-score comparisons across systems combine differences in models, communication patterns, roles, and computation costs, making performance differences difficult to attribute to specific communication structures, role assignments, and information flows. To address this evaluation attribution problem, we introduce OpenMAS-GCom, a benchmark for diagnosing how these components affect G-MAS performance through controlled interventions. We represent systems through collaboration units, communication links, shared intermediate information, and execution rules. OpenMAS-GCom compares original systems with versions modified by changing one component while keeping tasks, models, prompts, and budget limits fixed. We rewire communication edges, remove specialist or critic agents, replace intermediate messages with incorrect content, and disable workers during execution. The benchmark evaluates 17 single-agent, ordinary multi-agent, and graph-enhanced configurations on 29 datasets across six domains. We add 400 G-MAS-Complex tasks requiring agents to combine information from multiple documents, resolve conflicting records, and return specified values with source identifiers. Experiments show larger mean losses after specialist removal than after critic removal, different performance degradation under incorrect messages and worker failures despite similar original scores, and different configurations achieving the highest accuracy and accuracy per token on G-MAS-Complex.

</details>


### [236] [MACE: Memory-Agent Co-Evolution with Adaptive Memory Graphs for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21533)
*Kairui Yang,Minghao An,Xunkai Li,Ziheng Yi,Zekai Chen,Guangyuan He,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MACE记忆-智能体协同演化框架，通过功能单元分组与关系连接组织多智能体协作记忆，并结合执行反馈自适应更新记忆结构与呈现格式选择，在八个基准上平均得分81.11%，优于最强基线SAGE的78.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统产生的协作轨迹记录了规划、验证与修复过程，复用这些过程需要保留动作前置条件和后续智能体所需输出。但现有记忆组织方式难以有效保留和检索这些复杂依赖关系，且指令与清单两种呈现格式下的最优记忆组合并不相同，因此需要一种能够协同演化记忆组织与智能体记忆使用方式的框架。

Method: 通过实证研究验证功能记忆单元分组可提升依赖保留、单元连接可提升联合检索，并发现最优单元组合随格式变化。基于此提出MACE框架：其MemGoG结构将功能单元表示为条件、动作、输出的相关子图，并通过支持、冲突、修复关系连接；MACE Loop在记忆预算内选择任务相关单元与关系，为每个智能体提供指令或清单格式，并记录所选单元、格式、智能体输出与任务结果，用于更新单元得分与关系以优化检索和后续格式选择。

Result: 在八个基准测试中，MACE以81.11%的平均得分超越十个基线方法，最强基线SAGE为78.97%。

Conclusion: 基于执行反馈的联合优化单元组合与呈现格式优于分开评分，记忆-智能体协同演化能有效提升LLM多智能体系统的协作过程复用能力。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems generate collaboration traces that record how agents plan tasks, verify intermediate results, and repair failures. Reusing these procedures requires preserving an action's prerequisites and the outputs needed by subsequent agents. Our empirical studies show that grouping these dependencies into functional memory units improves their retention, while connecting units increases retrieval of the units and links jointly required by a task. The preferred combination of units also changes between instructions and checklists, even when each combination's content is fixed across formats. Updating choices from the outcomes of each combination and format pairing outperforms scoring combinations and formats separately. These findings motivate MACE, a memory-agent co-evolution framework that adapts memory organization and agent memory use through execution feedback. Its MemGoG structure represents functional units as subgraphs of related conditions, actions, and outputs, connecting them through support, conflict, and repair relations. MACE Loop selects task-relevant units and relations within a memory budget and provides each agent with instructions or checklists for its current operation. It records the selected units, presentation formats, agent outputs, and task outcomes to update unit scores and relations for retrieval and inform subsequent presentation choices. Across eight benchmarks, MACE outperforms ten baselines with an average score of 81.11%, compared with 78.97% for the strongest baseline, SAGE.

</details>


### [237] [OneBid: A Unified Auto-Bidding Foundation Model for Diverse oCPX Advertising Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21550)
*Yewen Li,Peng Jiang,Yitian Li,Pengfei Lv,Xialong Liu,Peng Jiang,Qingpeng Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OneBid：一个面向oCPX广告的统一自动出价基础模型，通过双目标条件化、序列级MoE骨干和离线策略优化，在快手全量部署并取得+2.2% ADVV提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动出价领域从规则控制、强化学习演进到Decision Transformer等生成式方法，但这些方法与当前主流的oCPX范式日益不匹配。oCPX涵盖注册、购买等异构场景，每个场景单独建模，导致流程碎片化，且缺乏跨场景建模的探索。受LLM等基础模型启发，作者希望将多个oCPX场景统一到一个模型中，但面临三大挑战：多目标控制、严格延迟下的可扩展容量、以及安全的离线策略改进。

Method: 提出OneBid统一自动出价基础模型：1）在Decision Transformer基础上，将单一Return-to-Go条件扩展为两个原子信号——用于转化价值的Return-to-Go和用于成本率的Cost-to-Go，并加入价值感知的下一动作预测正则；2）设计序列级Mixture-of-Experts架构，共享专家编码跨场景知识、稀疏路由专家捕获场景特定模式，实现低延迟下随模型规模和数据的一致扩展；3）后训练阶段采用Critic引导的相对离线策略优化（CROP），用学习的critic对候选动作做组内相对打分，避免GRPO式微调的不安全在线探索，并约束策略偏移以降低OOD风险。

Result: 经在线A/B测试验证并在快手全量部署，OneBid在oCPX广告上整体取得+2.2%的ADVV提升，在ROAS场景中峰值达+13.1%。

Conclusion: OneBid验证了以基础模型范式统一异构oCPX出价场景的可行性与有效性，通过双目标条件化、MoE扩展和安全离线策略优化，在严格延迟约束下实现了跨场景知识复用与场景自适应，并在真实工业系统中取得显著线上收益。

Abstract: Auto-bidding is central to computational advertising, where strategies must maximize advertisers' conversion value under economic constraints. It has evolved from rule-based controllers to reinforcement learning and generative methods such as Decision Transformer (DT). Yet these methods increasingly mismatch the prevailing optimized cost-per-X (oCPX) paradigm, which spans heterogeneous scenarios (e.g., registration, purchase), each served by a separate model, leading to fragmented pipelines and underexploring cross-scenario modeling. Inspired by foundation models like LLMs, unifying these oCPX scenarios into one model raises three challenges: multi-objective control, scalable capacity under strict latency, and safe offline policy improvement. We present OneBid, a unified auto-bidding foundation model that learns a reusable backbone from heterogeneous oCPX logs and adapts it to scenario-specific deployments via offline post-training. Building on DT, OneBid extends single Return-to-Go conditioning to two atomic signals, Return-to-Go for conversion value and Cost-to-Go for cost ratio, plus value-aware regularization on next-action prediction. To absorb distributional heterogeneity, we design a sequence-level Mixture-of-Experts architecture, where shared experts encode cross-scenario knowledge and sparsely-routed experts capture scenario-specific patterns at low latency, yielding consistent scaling with model size and data. During post-training, we align the backbone with scenario preferences via Critic-guided Relative Offline Policy optimization (CROP): a learned critic scores candidate actions group-relatively, avoiding the unsafe online exploration of GRPO-style fine-tuning while constraining policy shift to reduce OOD risk. Validated via online A/B tests and fully deployed at Kuaishou, OneBid delivers an overall +2.2% ADVV gain on oCPX Ads, peaking at +13.1% in the ROAS scenario.

</details>


### [238] [ResNLS: An Improved Model for Stock Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.01020)
*Yuanzhe Jia,Ali Anaissi,Basem Suleiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ResNLS混合模型，通过ResNet提取相邻股价依赖作为残差，结合LSTM预测股价，ResNLS-5性能优于基线至少20%，并在量化回测中有效避险盈利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 股价预测难度大，现有研究多忽视股票价格之间不同程度依赖关系这一问题。作者希望通过对相邻股价依赖性的建模来提升预测精度。

Method: ResNLS由ResNet与LSTM组成。ResNet作为特征提取器识别股价间的依赖关系，LSTM在结合这些被视为残差的依赖信息基础上分析原始时间序列数据。实验对比不同输入窗口，发现以前5个连续交易日收盘价为输入（ResNLS-5）效果最佳，并构建量化交易框架进行回测。

Result: ResNLS-5相比当前最先进基线至少有20%的提升；基于其预测的交易策略能在股价下跌时减少损失，在上涨期获得收益。

Conclusion: 强调相邻股价依赖性的ResNLS-5模型可有效提升股价预测性能，并在实际量化交易回测中帮助投资者规避风险、获取利润。

Abstract: Stock prices forecasting has always been a challenging task. Although many research projects try to address the problem, few of them pay attention to the varying degrees of dependencies between stock prices. In this paper, we introduce a hybrid model that improves the prediction of stock prices by emphasizing the dependencies between adjacent stock prices. The proposed model, ResNLS, is mainly composed of two neural architectures, ResNet and LSTM. ResNet serves as a feature extractor to identify dependencies between stock prices, while LSTM analyzes the initial time series data with the combination of dependencies, which are considered as residuals. Our experiment reveals that when the closing price data for the previous 5 consecutive trading days is used as input, the performance of the model (ResNLS-5) is optimal compared to those with other inputs. Furthermore, ResNLS-5 demonstrates at least a 20% improvement over current state-of-the-art baselines. To verify whether ResNLS-5 can help clients effectively avoid risks and earn profits in the stock market, we construct a quantitative trading framework for back testing. The result shows that the trading strategy based on ResNLS-5 predictions can successfully mitigate losses during declining stock prices and generate profits in periods of rising stock prices.

</details>


### [239] [On Repulsive and Attractive Teachers: Separating Correctness from Behavior in Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21561)
*Anton Baumann,Akmal Ashirmatov,Leo Schmidt-Traub,Frederike Lübeck,Jonas Hübotter,Thomas Kleine Buening,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究on-policy自蒸馏中特权信息带来的行为偏移问题，发现吸引式与排斥式自蒸馏会产生相反的行为变化，而对比式自蒸馏（吸引正确解教师+排斥错误解教师）可抵消这些行为偏移，保留更纯粹的 token 级正确性信号，提升推理性能且保持响应长度稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy自蒸馏通过特权信息提供密集的token级监督，但特权信息不仅改变教师"知道什么"，还会改变其"行为方式"，从而将正确性相关信号与意外的行为偏移纠缠在一起。作者希望探究这种效应，并寻找能分离出纯正确性信号的方法。

Method: 在推理任务上对比三种自蒸馏目标：(1) 吸引式自蒸馏——向特权教师靠拢；(2) 排斥式自蒸馏——远离特权教师；(3) 对比式自蒸馏——向正确解条件教师吸引、同时从错误解条件教师排斥。与前人将此信号与GRPO结合不同，作者单独隔离出自蒸馏目标进行研究，并在非思考型、仅指令型以及已思考型模型上测试。

Result: 吸引与排斥目标会引发强烈且相反的行为偏移：吸引抑制探索性推理，产生更短、更自信的响应；排斥增加响应长度，可能触发模型意外切换到潜在思考模式，且最终变得不稳定。对比式自蒸馏中两个教师共享的行为偏移大部分相互抵消，留下更直接反映正确性的token级信号；在各类模型上均提升推理性能并保持稳定的响应长度。

Conclusion: 对比式自蒸馏通过组合相反方向的行为偏移，能有效剥离特权信息带来的行为干扰，保留纯粹的正确性学习信号，从而在多种模型设置下稳定提升推理能力，为自蒸馏方法设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation provides dense, token-level supervision by conditioning a model on privileged information and distilling the resulting teacher distribution back into the model. However, privileged information can change not only what the teacher knows, but also how it behaves, entangling correctness-relevant learning signals with unintended behavioral shifts. We study this effect in reasoning tasks by contrasting attractive self-distillation, which moves the model toward a privileged teacher, with repulsive self-distillation, which moves it away from a privileged teacher. We find that both objectives can induce strong and opposing behavioral shifts: attraction suppresses exploratory reasoning and promotes shorter, more confident responses, whereas repulsion increases response length, can trigger unintended switches into a model's latent thinking mode, and ultimately becomes unstable. Motivated by these observations, we study contrastive self-distillation, which combines attraction toward a correct-solution-conditioned teacher with repulsion from an incorrect-solution-conditioned teacher. In contrast to prior work that combines such distillation signals with a GRPO objective, we isolate the self-distillation objective and study its behavior on its own. We find that the shared behavioral shifts of the two teachers largely cancel, leaving a token-level signal that more directly reflects correctness. Across non-thinking, instruct-only, and already-thinking models, this contrastive objective improves reasoning performance while maintaining stable response lengths.

</details>


### [240] [A Hybrid Computational Intelligence Framework for scRNA-seq Imputation: Integrating scRecover and Random Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16923)
*Ali Anaissi,Deshao Liu,Yuanzhe Jia,Weidong Huang,Widad Alyassine,Junaid Akram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCR-MF两阶段scRNA-seq数据插补流程，先检测dropout再插补，兼顾准确性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞RNA测序存在普遍的dropout事件，掩盖生物学信号，需要可靠且可解释的插补方法。

Method: 模块化两阶段流程：用scRecover进行有原则的dropout检测，再用missForest进行稳健的非参数插补。

Result: 在公开与模拟数据集上，SCR-MF性能多数情况下可比或优于现有插补方法，保持生物学保真度与透明度；运行时间分析显示其在精度与计算效率之间具有竞争力。

Conclusion: SCR-MF适用于中等规模单细胞数据集，是一种稳健、可解释的插补方案。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) enables transcriptomic profiling at cellular resolution but suffers from pervasive dropout events that obscure biological signals. We present SCR-MF, a modular two-stage workflow that combines principled dropout detection using scRecover with robust non-parametric imputation via missForest. Across public and simulated datasets, SCR-MF achieves robust and interpretable performance comparable to or exceeding existing imputation methods in most cases, while preserving biological fidelity and transparency. Runtime analysis demonstrates that SCR-MF provides a competitive balance between accuracy and computational efficiency, making it suitable for mid-scale single-cell datasets.

</details>


### [241] [Trading Depth for Time in Recurrent Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21605)
*Zeyi Huang,Xuehai He,Yong Jae Lee,Yelong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究循环Transformer中增加计算深度应投入更多时间步还是更大物理深度的问题，提出少参数的时间步（思考token）可高效逼近双倍深度模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过时间循环增加计算深度，但不确定额外计算应分配在更多循环时间步还是更大物理层数上，需要一个受控对比方法。

Method: 提出Latent Recurrent Transformers（LRT）：在相邻词表token间插入潜在思考token，共享同一L层骨干参数，使每token解码时执行2L个Transformer块。将其与无思考token的2L层LRT对比，在16层与20层MoE NanoChat骨干上进行实验。

Result: 一个思考token使浅层模型与双倍深度模型差距缩小到0.006和0.004 bits per byte，恢复67%和81%的提升，同时总参数量约减少48%。

Conclusion: 在循环Transformer中，时间步上的"思考"是比增加物理深度更参数高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Recurrent Transformers increase computational depth through temporal recurrence, feeding each token's high-level hidden state into the computation of the next. This raises a natural question: is additional computation better spent on more temporal steps or greater physical depth? We investigate this question using Latent Recurrent Transformers (LRTs), which retain one backbone forward pass per vocabulary token during decoding and provide a controlled setting for comparing these two ways of adding computation. Specifically, we insert a latent thought token between consecutive vocabulary tokens. Each thought token passes through the same $L$ layers as a vocabulary token, sharing the backbone parameters and providing an additional stage of hidden-state refinement before predicting the next token. We compare this $L$-layer LRT against a $2L$-layer LRT without thought tokens. Both execute $2L$ Transformer blocks per vocabulary token during decoding, but the thought-token model uses fewer parameters. On 16- and 20-layer mixture-of-experts NanoChat backbones, one thought token brings the shallower model within 0.006 and 0.004 bits per byte of its double-depth counterpart, recovering 67% and 81% of the improvement with approximately 48% fewer total parameters. These results suggest that temporal thinking offers a parameter-efficient alternative to increasing physical depth in recurrent Transformers.

</details>


### [242] [Making Latent Evolution Explicit: Operator-Structured Transitions for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27259)
*Xiaoxiao Lu,Yunlong Dong,Jiahao Shi,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LEON（Latent Evolution Operator Network），将潜在世界动作模型中的"转移实现"视为独立于预测表示与预测-策略耦合的架构选择，通过上下文调制的算子传播与加性强迫建模潜在演化，提升闭环性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）多在潜在表示空间预测任务相关场景状态演化，但潜在转移通常由基于 Transformer 的预测器实现，其归纳结构偏向 token 交互而非时间演化。作者认为"转移实现"是一个应与预测表示、预测-策略耦合相区分的架构选择。

Method: 提出 Latent Evolution Operator Network（LEON）：在学习到的可观空间内，通过上下文调制的算子基传播与加性强迫项建模潜在演化。理论依据为受控 Koopman 生成元视角，将上下文相关的转移变异性组织在共享演化算子结构周围，同时保留加性变化的补充路径。

Result: 在受控动力系统上验证了该演化特异的归纳偏置，以及算子传播与强迫项的互补作用；在两种以不同方式将潜在预测整合进策略的 WAM 形式中，LEON 均提升闭环性能与鲁棒性，且在完全替换转移模块时仍然有效。

Conclusion: 结果确立了转移实现是潜在世界动作模型中一个具有实质影响的架构选择，算子式演化建模范式优于以 token 交互为中心的预测器。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies by predicting how task-relevant scene states may evolve under interaction. Recent WAMs increasingly perform such prediction in latent representation spaces, avoiding full appearance-level generation while preserving control-relevant information. Yet latent transitions are commonly realized with Transformer-based predictors whose inductive structure is centered on token interaction rather than temporal evolution. We study transition realization as an architectural choice distinct from predictive representation and prediction-policy coupling. We introduce the Latent Evolution Operator Network (LEON), which models latent evolution in a learned observable space through context-modulated operator-based propagation and additive forcing. Grounded in the controlled Koopman generator view of evolution, LEON organizes context-dependent transition variation around a shared evolution-operator structure while retaining a complementary path for additive change. Controlled dynamical systems verify the resulting evolution-specific inductive bias and the complementary roles of operator propagation and forcing. Across two WAM formulations that integrate latent prediction into the policy differently, LEON improves closed-loop performance and robustness while remaining effective under full transition replacement. These results establish transition realization as a consequential architectural choice in latent WAMs.

</details>


### [243] [Riemannian Neural Hamiltonian Flows: Geodesic Symplectic Transport and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21647)
*Vincent Souveton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出黎曼神经哈密顿流（Riemannian Neural Hamiltonian Flows），将黎曼流形的固定动能、可学习标量势与显式测地蛙跳积分器结合，并给出可解释性理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈密顿归一化流因相空间映射可逆且保体积而具吸引力，但多数神经构造局限于欧氏空间，缺乏黎曼流形上的推广与可解释性分析。

Method: 在黎曼流形上组合固定动能项、学习的标量势与显式测地蛙跳积分器；理论上定义可归一化势对应的隐式分布，分析位置边缘沿相对得分加速的机制、匹配势（隐式分布即目标）的解释，并通过局部调和分析推广到一般目标各模态，刻画学习势与匹配势之差为基分布残差记忆与模型偏差之和。

Result: 在欧氏、双曲与球面空间的数值实验中，采样质量与数值开销与黎曼连续归一化流相当，并验证了学习势的可解释性。

Conclusion: 黎曼神经哈密顿流为流形上的生成建模提供了可逆保体积且势函数可解释的框架，当位置基分布宽于目标时可将其转移至动量从而使学习势与匹配势一致。

Abstract: Hamiltonian normalizing flows are attractive generative models because their phase-space maps are invertible and volume preserving, but most neural constructions are formulated in Euclidean space. We introduce Riemannian Neural Hamiltonian Flows, which combine the fixed kinetic energy of a Riemannian manifold, a learned scalar potential, and an explicit geodesic leapfrog integrator. Our analysis explains how the learned Hamiltonian can be made interpretable. Every normalizable potential defines an implicit profile, and the position marginal initially accelerates along the relative score between that profile and the base. The matched potential is the interpretable specialization for which the implicit profile is the target. In the isotropic Gaussian case, the mechanism corresponds to a phase-space rotation. A local harmonic analysis extends this result around each mode of a general target on a manifold. The gap between the learned and the matched potential is the sum of a residual memory of the base and a bias of the model, and the two potentials agree when the position base has been transferred to the momentum. This can be achieved when the former is broader than the target. Numerical experiments on Euclidean, hyperbolic, and spherical spaces show competitive sample quality and numerical cost against a Riemannian continuous normalizing flow, and confirm the interpretability of the learned potential.

</details>


### [244] [Beyond Gaussian Worlds: Latent Geometry Matters for JEPAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21656)
*Léo Nicollier,Enric Meinhardt-Llopis,Marc Pic,Pablo Musé,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将分布匹配式JEPA的可辨识性分析从欧氏空间推广到嵌入黎曼流形的潜变量，证明球面均匀分布在正交变换下可线性恢复，打破了高斯唯一性，并给出更紧的近似恢复界与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有JEPA工作依靠将表示匹配到预设目标分布（如各向同性高斯、超球面均匀分布）来防止表示坍缩；Klindt等（2026）在欧氏假设下证明了高斯目标是唯一能保证线性恢复潜变量的分布。作者希望检验这一"高斯唯一性"是否依赖于欧氏几何假设，探索非欧潜几何下是否存在其他可线性恢复的分布。

Method: 将分析推广到支撑在嵌入黎曼流形上的潜变量，推导在何种潜几何与正样本对动态条件下，对齐加精确分布匹配能够保证线性恢复；重点分析潜变量在球面均匀分布且表示匹配同一球面分布的情形，证明最优表示可恢复潜状态至正交变换；进一步推导近似恢复界，并与高斯情形比较紧致性；在高斯、球面与环面潜空间以及高维Clifford环面世界上进行实验。

Result: 当潜变量球面均匀分布且表示匹配同一球面分布时，每个最优表示都能在正交变换意义下恢复潜状态；由此说明高斯唯一性并非分布匹配式JEPA的普适性质，非欧潜几何可容纳其他线性可恢复分布。所导出的近似恢复界在球面情形下严格优于高斯情形。实验中，几何相容的目标在优化成功时带来更好的线性恢复，而不匹配的目标会扭曲潜结构；该优势在高维Clifford环面世界上依然存在。

Conclusion: 分布匹配式JEPA的线性可恢复性取决于潜几何与目标分布的相容性，而非高斯分布本身；选择与潜流形几何匹配的目标分布可提升线性恢复质量，这为JEPA目标分布设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Recent Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) prevent representation collapse by constraining learned representations to follow a prescribed target distribution, such as an isotropic Gaussian or the uniform distribution on a hypersphere. Klindt et al. (2026) showed that, under their Euclidean assumptions, matching a Gaussian target can recover Gaussian latent variables up to a linear transformation, and that the Gaussian is the unique distribution with this guarantee. We extend their analysis to latent variables supported on embedded Riemannian manifolds and derive conditions on the latent geometry and positive-pair dynamics under which alignment and exact distribution matching guarantee linear recovery. In particular, when the latent variables are uniformly distributed on a sphere and the representations are matched to the same spherical distribution, every optimal representation recovers the latent state up to an orthogonal transformation. This shows that Gaussian uniqueness is not a universal property of distribution-matched JEPAs: non-Euclidean latent geometries can admit other linearly recoverable distributions. We further derive an approximate-recovery bound that is strictly tighter for the spherical world than for the Gaussian world. Experiments on Gaussian, spherical, and toroidal latent spaces show that geometrically compatible targets yield better linear recovery when optimization succeeds, whereas mismatched targets distort the latent structure. This advantage persists in high-dimensional Clifford-torus worlds.

</details>


### [245] [Multi-Domain Clustering via Measure Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21664)
*Rafael Pereira Eufrazio,Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma,Charles Casimiro Cavalcante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于测度量化的多域聚类通用框架，通过最小化概率度量学习共享聚类原型，并利用小批量优化实现可扩展的聚类拟合与分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类是数据分析的基础任务，通常依赖K-means等基于质心的方法。然而面对来自多个域的数据时，如何学习共享的聚类原型并兼顾计算与内存可扩展性仍具挑战。

Method: 给定多个域的样本，学习一组共享的聚类原型，通过最小化每个域的概率测度与原型测度之间的概率度量（如Sinkhorn散度或最大均值差异MMD）。数据点分配采用最近质心或最优传输的协作策略，并引入小批量优化策略以降低内存和计算成本。

Result: 在涵盖图像、音频和传感器数据的5个多域基准上，基于Sinkhorn的方法一致优于经典聚类和多域聚类基线，且该优势在扩展到数十万样本时依然保持。

Conclusion: 该工作为多域聚类提供了通用且可扩展的框架，验证了测度量化与最优传输策略的有效性，并在大规模数据上展现了良好性能。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental task in data analysis, typically addressed through centroid-based methods such as K-means. In this work, we present a general framework for multi-domain clustering via measure quantization: given samples from multiple domains, we learn a shared set of cluster prototypes by minimizing a probability metric, such as the Sinkhorn divergence or the Maximum Mean Discrepancy, between each domain's probability measure and the measure of prototypes. Data points are then assigned to clusters either via nearest centroid, or via optimal transport, a collaborative strategy that couples all samples within a domain. A mini-batch optimization strategy makes both fitting and assignment scalable, reducing memory and computational cost while preserving clustering performance. Experimental results on 5 multi-domain benchmarks spanning image, audio and sensor data show that our Sinkhorn-based method consistently outperforms classical and multi-domain clustering baselines, and that this advantage persists when scaling to hundreds of thousands of samples.

</details>


### [246] [Optimization Geometry of Equivalent Brownian RKHS Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21693)
*Mahdi Mohammadigohari,Gustau Camps-Valls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究等价有限参数化（节点、增量、谱坐标）在布朗RKHS中如何在不改变所表示函数与内在正则项的前提下，导致不同的优化几何与算法行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一函数类可由多种有限参数化表示，其内在范数相同，但会诱导不同的优化算法与优化行为。作者希望在一个可控的有限布朗RKHS模型中，隔离并刻画这种"坐标效应"，明确参数化选择对优化几何、条件数以及Adam等自适应优化器的影响。

Method: 构建有限布朗RKHS上的节点坐标、增量坐标与谱坐标三种参数化，利用经典有限元、RKHS插值、布朗协方差与混合边界DCT恒等式，使共享假设类、布朗能量、逼近算子与坐标映射显式化。在映射初始化、相同标量步长与相同小批量下比较GD/SGD轨迹，并分析增量GD的欧拉步长因子、布朗正则最小二乘的条件数界以及Adam的正交等变群。以Float64在五个网格上进行数值验证。

Result: 在映射初始化、标量步长与小批量相同的条件下，节点GD/SGD与谱GD/SGD具有完全相同的映射轨迹；增量GD是常量布朗/Sobolev度量的显式欧拉步，步长含因子1/h。对布朗正则最小二乘，κ₂(H_inc) ≤ 1 + A/ρ，且当A、ρ固定并满足给定归一化时与网格分辨率G无关。在标准Adam约定下，通用正交等变群恰为带符号的置换群，而块DCT-VIII变换不属于该群。五个网格的Float64实验数值验证了有限恒等式、映射单层与递归轨迹、条件数预测以及与定理一致的Adam分离现象。

Conclusion: 不同的等价有限参数化会在保持所表示函数、内在正则化器和逼近空间不变的情况下，产生不同的优化几何与算法行为；通过在受控布朗RKHS中显式分离这些坐标效应，论文为理解参数化选择对优化（尤其对条件数与Adam等自适应方法）的影响提供了理论工具与数值证据。

Abstract: Equivalent finite parameterizations can represent the same functions and intrinsic norm yet induce different optimization algorithms. We study this effect in a controlled finite Brownian RKHS with nodal, increment, and spectral coordinates. Classical finite-element, RKHS-interpolation, Brownian-covariance, and mixed-boundary DCT identities make the shared hypothesis class, Brownian energy, approximation operator, and coordinate maps explicit. Our main results concern the optimization geometry of this fixed model. With mapped initialization, identical scalar steps, and identical minibatches, nodal and spectral GD/SGD have exactly the same mapped trajectories. Increment GD is an explicit Euler step for the constant Brownian/Sobolev metric, with factor $1/h$. For Brownian-regularized least squares, $κ_2(\mathbf H_{\mathrm{inc}})\le1+A/ρ$, independently of grid resolution $G$ for fixed $A$, $ρ>0$, and the stated normalization. Under the stated standard-Adam convention, the universal orthogonal equivariance group is exactly the signed permutations; the block DCT-VIII transform is not one. Float64 tests over five grids numerically verify the finite identities, mapped one-layer and recursive trajectories, conditioning predictions, and theorem-matched Adam separation. Thus coordinate effects are isolated without changing the represented functions, intrinsic regularizer, or approximation space.

</details>


### [247] [SpecQuant: Speculative Decoding with Multi-Parent Quantization for Adaptive LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21704)
*Harish KB,Jagadeeswaran M,Pradheep P,Yuvanesh S,Sivakumar T*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpecQuant 是一个免训练框架，结合投机解码与多父量化，实现 LLM 的自适应高效推理，在保持精度的同时获得 35-43% 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费级硬件上本地运行大语言模型受限于算力和内存。现有加速技术（量化、投机解码、自适应推理）虽有显著提速，但通常需要重新训练、针对特定架构调优或依赖草稿模型，部署门槛高。

Method: SpecQuant 从共享基座模型派生出多个量化变体（INT4、FP8、FP16），并结合投机解码与多父量化。系统根据预测的查询复杂度动态路由：简单或事实性任务使用轻量变体，复杂推理或长上下文输入使用全精度模型。共享权重设计确保了投机解码的足够 token 接受率，避免使用独立草稿父模型带来的兼容性问题。

Result: 基于 Qwen2.5 系列模型，在 MMLU、AlpacaEval 和 GSM8K 数据集/基准上评估，实现 35-43% 的加速，精度下降不超过 2%。

Conclusion: SpecQuant 无需特殊基础设施或专业知识，即可在多种硬件上实现实用的端侧 LLM 部署。

Abstract: Running large language models (LLMs) locally continues to be limited by restrictions of compute and memory on consumer hardware. The popular acceleration technologies, such as quantization, speculative decoding, and adaptive inferencing, offer substantial speed boosts but usually necessitate retraining, per architecture tuning, or draft models. SpecQuant is a trainingfree framework, that combines speculative decoding with multiparent quantization to perform adaptive, efficient inference of LLMs. SpecQuant derives multiple quantized variants (INT4, FP8, FP16) from a shared base model, and dynamically routes queries based on predicted complexity; lightweight variants are used for simple or factual tasks, and full-precision models are used for complex reasoning tasks or long-context inputs. The shared-weight design of SpecQuant ensures sufficient token acceptance for speculative decoding without compatibility issues using separate draft parent models. We evaluate SpecQuant on Qwen2.5 based models on the MMLU, AlpacaEval, and GSM8K datasets, or benchmarks, demonstrating 35-43% speedups without degrading accuracy greater than 2%, substantial within the LLM community. SpecQuant enables practical on-device LLM deployment across diverse hardware without special infrastructure or expertise.

</details>


### [248] [GEM-MPC: Balancing Exploration and Exploitation through Expert-Guided Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21735)
*Alvaro Serra-Gomez,Thomas Moerland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GEM-MPC，一种基于MPPI的强化学习方法，通过策略与规划器协同及门控先验蒸馏提升高维连续控制探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维连续控制中的有效探索是强化学习的核心难题。基于规划的方法结合在线规划与学习到的策略和价值函数，但训练中组件易失配：学习到的采样策略可能偏离规划器行为，且回放中的规划分布随模型和价值函数演进而变得陈旧。重分析可刷新目标但计算代价高昂。

Method: GEM-MPC基于MPPI，在规划中结合一个克隆规划器的策略与一个围绕其探索的KL正则化策略，提供互补的利用与引导探索。同时提出门控先验蒸馏（Gated Prior Distillation），仅在存储的规划分布比当前先验提供更好目标时选择性学习，从而降低陈旧规划数据影响，且无需完整重分析。

Result: 在连续控制基准上，GEM-MPC在更低计算预算下持续优于现有基于规划的基线方法。

Conclusion: GEM-MPC通过改善规划与学习的交互，有效缓解策略与规划器失配及陈旧规划数据问题，在低计算预算下实现更优性能。

Abstract: Effective exploration in high-dimensional continuous control remains a central challenge in reinforcement learning. Planning-based methods address this by combining online planning with learned policies and value functions, but their components can become misaligned during training: learned sampling policies may diverge from planner behavior, while planning distributions stored in replay become stale as the model and value function evolve. Reanalysis can refresh these targets, but at substantial computational cost. We propose GEM-MPC, an MPPI-based reinforcement learning method that improves the interaction between planning and learning. GEM-MPC uses MPPI to combine a policy trained to clone the planner with a KL-regularized policy that explores around it, providing complementary exploitation and guided exploration within planning. We further introduce Gated Prior Distillation, which selectively learns from stored planning distributions only when they provide a better target than the current prior, reducing the impact of stale planning data without requiring full reanalysis. Across continuous-control benchmarks, GEM-MPC consistently outperforms existing planning-based baselines under lower computational budgets.

</details>


### [249] [GraphSkillEvo: Evolutionary Optimization of Graph-Structured Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21749)
*Rui Sun,Zhi Zheng,Zhenkun Wang,Zhichao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将技能表示为图结构的自然语言工件，并用进化优化框架GraphSkillEvo优化，提升LLM智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能优化方法将技能表示为无结构自然语言指令，存在两大问题：一是缺乏显式工作流级指导且冗余严重，难以执行；二是无约束的自然语言搜索空间过大，导致优化效率低。

Method: 将技能表示为图结构自然语言工件，节点为执行步骤及其操作指导，有向边表示依赖上下文的步骤转移；在此基础上提出GraphSkillEvo，一个基于种群的进化优化框架，包含针对图结构技能的变异与交叉算子，通过维护多个候选技能并组合有效组件来探索结构化技能空间。

Result: 在五个智能体基准测试上，GraphSkillEvo持续优于强基线SkillOpt，在GPT-5.4-nano上平均准确率提升4.01%，在GPT-5.4上提升1.76%。

Conclusion: 图结构技能表示能提供清晰的工作流级指导，并支持更广更全面的进化式优化，优于纯LLM迭代自精炼方法。

Abstract: Skills can improve the performance of Large Language Model (LLM) agents by providing task-specific procedural guidance, while skill optimization further improves their effectiveness through iterative refinement. However, existing skill optimization methods typically represent skills as unstructured natural-language instructions, creating two key challenges: 1) Unstructured skills often lack explicit workflow-level guidance and contain substantial redundancy, making them difficult for LLMs to execute; 2) the vast search space of unconstrained natural-language skills makes skill optimization ineffective. To address these challenges, we propose representing skills as graph-structured natural-language artifacts. In graph-structured skills, each node represents an execution step together with its operational guidance, while directed edges encode context-dependent transitions between steps. Compared to unstructured skills, graph-structured skills can provide clear workflow-level guidance. Moreover, the proposed graph-structured skill can also facilitate skill optimization. Building on this structured representation, we introduce GraphSkillEvo, a population-based evolutionary optimization framework with mutation and crossover operators for graph-structured skills. By maintaining multiple candidate skills and combining effective components, GraphSkillEvo enables broader and more comprehensive exploration of the structured skill space than purely LLM-based iterative self-refinement. Extensive experiments across five agent benchmarks demonstrate that GraphSkillEvo consistently outperforms the strong skill optimization baseline SkillOpt, improving average accuracy by 4.01% on GPT-5.4-nano and 1.76% on GPT-5.4. Our code is available at https://github.com/ruisun7/GraphSkillEvo.

</details>


### [250] [Bilevel Optimization of Topology and Hyperparameters (BOTH)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21758)
*Suryanarayanan Manoj Sanu,Miguel Anibal Bessa,Alejandro Marcos Aragón*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过对拓扑优化本身进行自动微分来获取"超梯度"，从而在优化过程中同步调节大量超参数，仅需一两步优化即可获得足够信息，且能以相当于几次标准拓扑优化的代价扩展到数千个超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑优化虽能自动化设计，但实践中充满"魔法数字"（超参数），其调优严重影响结果，通常需要深厚的专业知识与大量试错。代理辅助超参数优化虽可作为替代方案，但需通过精心的问题建模严格限制超参数数量，因此需要一种能高效处理大量超参数的方法。

Method: 提出对拓扑优化本身进行自动微分，从而得到"超梯度"，使超参数能够与主优化过程协同调节。研究发现仅评估一两步拓扑优化就足以提供有效信息，并证明该方法可良好扩展至数千个超参数。

Result: 在应力约束问题和柔度问题上验证了该方法，其中柔度问题采用了密度场的神经参数化。方法可扩展至数千个超参数，代价仅相当于几次标准拓扑优化运行。

Conclusion: 通过自动微分拓扑优化获取超梯度，可高效同步调节大量超参数，为拓扑优化中超参数的自动化调优提供了可行且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Topology optimization (TO) represents a significant step towards automating the design process: given a working simulation, TO can produce a viable prototype at the press of a button by differentiating the simulation and iteratively improving the design. In practice, however, TO is riddled with ``magic numbers''---hyperparameters whose tuning significantly affects the outcome. Finding the right values typically requires not only deep problem-specific knowledge but also extensive trial-and-error. While practitioners can use surrogate-assisted hyperparameter optimization as an alternative, this approach requires strictly limiting the number of hyperparameters through careful problem formulation. Here, we propose differentiating TO itself using automatic differentiation. This yields ``hypergradients'' that allow us to tune these hyperparameters in tandem with the primary optimization. We show that evaluating just one or two steps of TO is sufficiently informative and that the method scales favorably to thousands of hyperparameters at an expense comparable to only a few standard TO runs.
